Conclusion: 该工作确立了TFLT作为制造兼容平台用于可扩展光子量子电路的可行性，为经典与量子光子功能的单片共集成铺平了道路。

Abstract: Thin-film lithium tantalate (TFLT) has emerged as a promising integrated photonic platform owing to its low photorefractive noise, high optical damage threshold, and reduced birefringence, attracting increasing interest for scalable photonic technologies. Here, to the best of our knowledge, we demonstrate the first quantum light source with TFLT via spontaneous four-wave mixing, bridging the gap between the rapidly advancing classical TFLT ecosystem and integrated quantum photonics. The fabricated microring exhibits a free spectral range of 350~GHz and an optical quality factor of $10^6$, enabling efficient cavity-enhanced nonlinear interactions. Correlated photon pairs are generated across the telecom band from 1510 to 1570~nm, with a photon pair generation rate of 24 $\mathrm{MHz/mW^{2}}$ at a wavelength of 1535.04 nm. The source delivers strongly antibunched heralded single photons with $g^{(2)}_{H}(0)=0.071\pm0.004$ at a heralding rate of 170 kHz, while the unheralded statistics yield $g^{(2)}(0)=1.93 \pm 0.05$, indicating near-single-temporal-mode emission. Energy-time entanglement is further confirmed by a raw two-photon interference visibility of $92.55\pm0.94\%$, well above the Bell-inequality violation threshold. These results establish TFLT as a manufacturing-compatible platform for scalable photonic quantum circuits, paving the way for the monolithic co-integration of classical and quantum photonic functionalities.

</details>


### [101] [Correlated Quantum Sensing at the Seemingly Classical Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01673)
*K. P. Athulya,Sreenath K. Manikandan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用高Q值谐振量子探测器在弱相互作用辐射场（如引力波）中探测量子统计特性的方法，提出了基于热态初始化（受辐射热测量术启发）的关联计数、零差和外差探测策略，并针对三个临界功能的谐振质量探测器提出了对称相关器统计检验方案，以在桌面实验中揭示引力子的互补量子噪声特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测弱相互作用辐射场的不可分量子及其量子统计特性极具挑战性，但高质量的谐振探测器在此极限下具有统计优势，可用于探索看似经典极限下（大量量子入射）的量子效应，特别是为引力子量子特性的桌面实验探测提供可行方案。

Method: 采用高Q值谐振量子探测器，在大量量子入射的经典极限下运行，使用受辐射热测量术启发的零均值热态初始化；提出关联计数、零差和外差探测策略；针对两个和三个临界功能的谐振质量探测器，设计对称相关器进行统计检验。

Result: 比较了量子光学中高Q值谐振探测器与量子引力中临界功能的谐振质量四极振荡器在辐射热测量术下的异同；所提出的对称相关器统计检验能够揭示引力子的互补量子噪声特性。

Conclusion: 高质量的谐振量子探测器在经典极限下仍可用于探测弱相互作用辐射场的量子统计效应，所提出的多探测器对称相关器方案有望在桌面实验中实现引力子量子特性的实验检验。

Abstract: It is a difficult task to detect the indivisible quanta of weakly interacting radiation fields, and even more challenging to probe their quantum statistics. Nevertheless, if barely functional high-quality resonant detectors are feasible for weakly interacting radiation fields, they do come with certain statistical advantages to probe quantum effects at the seemingly classical limit of a large number of quanta of the incoming radiation field. We present correlated counting, homodyne, and heterodyne detection strategies using high-quality resonant quantum harmonic detectors operating at this limit, initialized in bolometry-inspired zero-mean preparations such as thermal states. We compare the bolometric regime of good resonant harmonic detectors in quantum optics to the bolometric regime of barely functional resonant mass quadrupole oscillators as detectors for quantum gravity. Simple statistical tests are proposed using symmetric correlators for two and three such barely functional resonant mass detectors that could reveal the complementary quantum noise characteristics of gravitons in tabletop experiments.

</details>


### [102] [Pauli-structured preconditioning for quantum linear system solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01733)
*Hantao Nie,Zhijian Lai,Dong An*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Pauli结构化的预条件子在量子线性系统求解器中的应用，发现通过代数重组Pauli展开可以降低预条件算子的Pauli系数权重，从而改善量子算法的归一化参数，而非仅改善经典条件数。数值实验验证了该方法能降低块编码诊断指标和随机化QLS的每样本深度代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明，在量子线性系统算法中，对系统矩阵和预条件子分别进行块编码后组合会引入归一化开销，导致预条件化的优势被抵消。本文探讨是否可以利用额外的代数结构（Pauli结构化表示）使预条件化在量子访问模型下有效。

Method: 利用系统矩阵和预条件子的Pauli结构化表示，通过代数重组Pauli乘积来构建预条件算子的Pauli展开，推导了重组后Pauli表示的大小和系数权重界限，并分析了其对直接块编码构造和随机化Pauli线性系统求解器的影响。

Result: 推导了重组Pauli表示的大小和系数权重界限；在有限维合成基准上的数值实验显示，该方法降低了范数感知的直接块编码诊断指标和随机化QLS的每样本深度代理。

Conclusion: Pauli结构化的预条件化可以降低QLS算法的有效复杂度参数，而不仅仅是改善经典条件数，为量子线性代数中预条件化的实际应用提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Preconditioning is a fundamental technique for accelerating classical linear system solvers, and understanding when its benefits persist in quantum linear system (QLS) solvers is important for assessing the practical resource requirements of quantum linear algebra. In QLS algorithms, however, the potential advantage of preconditioning may be offset by the normalization overhead incurred by composing separate block-encodings of the system matrix and the preconditioner, as observed in recent work. This limitation leaves open whether additional algebraic structure can make preconditioning effective in quantum access models. Motivated by this question, we show that Pauli-structured representations of both the system matrix and the preconditioner allow the preconditioned operator to be accessed through regrouped Pauli expansions. In this setting, algebraic regrouping of Pauli products can reduce the Pauli coefficient weight of the preconditioned operator, thereby altering the normalization parameters relevant to quantum algorithms. We derive explicit size and coefficient-weight bounds for the regrouped Pauli representations, and we trace their consequences for both direct block-encoding constructions and randomized Pauli linear system solvers. These results identify when Pauli-structured preconditioning can reduce the effective complexity parameters of QLS algorithms, rather than merely improving the classical condition number. Numerical experiments on a finite-dimensional synthetic benchmark show reductions in norm-aware direct block-encoding diagnostics and in the randomized QLS per-sample depth proxy.

</details>


### [103] [Revisiting the Quantum-Guided Cluster Algorithm: Improvements and Numerical Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01826)
*Peter J. Eder,Sarah Braun*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展的量子引导聚类算法（QGCA），通过引入次近邻（NNN）信息来改进Max-Cut问题的求解，在随机正则图和非简并tile-planted实例上表现出色，并给出了非简并实例的标度分析，最后提出了向相关引导马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛算法扩展的方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相关引导聚类算法（如QGCA）仅利用两点相关性指导聚类更新，忽略了更远距离的关联信息，限制了求解Max-Cut问题的性能提升。引入次近邻信息有望增强聚类构造的有效性，进而提升解的质量。

Method: 在QGCA框架基础上，扩展聚类构造过程，纳入次近邻（NNN）信息；在不同相关源（correlation sources）下进行实验评估；在随机正则图和非简并tile-planted实例上测试性能，并对非简并实例进行标度分析；提出向相关引导马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛算法的扩展方向。

Result: 引入NNN信息的扩展算法在非简并tile-planted实例上表现尤为出色；提供了非简并实例的标度分析结果；相关引导MCMC算法尚处于初步提出阶段，详细分析留待未来工作。

Conclusion: 通过引入次近邻信息有效提升了相关引导聚类算法在Max-Cut问题上的性能，特别是对非简并实例；相关引导MCMC算法是一个有前景的开放方向。

Abstract: We study correlation-guided cluster algorithms for solving the Max-Cut problem that iteratively try to improve solutions by updating clusters of nodes. Building on the recently proposed quantum-guided cluster algorithm (QGCA) [arXiv:2508.10656], which leverages precomputed two-point correlations to guide collective updates, we extend the cluster construction by incorporating next-nearest-neighbor (NNN) information. We evaluate this extension across different correlation sources on random regular graphs and non-degenerate tile-planted instances. Notably, we observe particularly strong performance on non-degenerate instances and provide a scaling analysis for this class. Finally, we outline an extension toward a correlation-guided Markov-chain Monte Carlo algorithm, whose detailed analysis remains an open direction for future work.

</details>


### [104] [Half the Interference, Most of the Answer: Approximate Quantum Simulation via Path-Sum Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01922)
*Sinan Pehlivanoglu,Srinivasan Iyengar,Amr Sabry*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出统计干扰采样框架，将量子电路模拟中的端点干扰作为可调度计算显式处理。通过在化学抽象机模型中用阈值规则跳过约50%的干扰反应，仍能在Deutsch-Jozsa、Grover搜索、Simon问题和Shor算法等基准电路上保持超过90%的输出精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电路经典模拟有两个代价来源：状态空间大小（指数级振幅）和干扰（计算历史在共同端点处的相干聚合）。现有工作多聚焦于前者，而后者作为独立瓶颈尚未被充分显式研究。本文旨在将干扰计算作为可调度的独立资源进行显式建模和近似。

Method: 基于化学抽象机（ChAM）建模，将加权路径贡献视为并发分子物种，干扰反应合并共享相同输出态的分量。引入阈值规则：一旦端点累积足够振幅即终止过程，丢弃剩余反应。在Deutsch-Jozsa、Grover搜索、Simon问题和小规模Shor周期查找电路上进行评估。

Result: 在大多数测试算法中，可跳过近50%的端点干扰反应，同时保持超过90%的输出精度。干扰算术是一种可结构化近似的资源，显式暴露后可支持路径求和、Pauli路径和张量网络模拟方法中的剪枝策略。

Conclusion: 该方法不改善最坏情况复杂度，也不作为通用模拟器，而是回答一个针对性问题：在仍能恢复有用输出分布的前提下，可以跳过多少干扰计算。结果表明干扰计算存在可近似性，为多种量子模拟方法的剪枝优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Classical simulation of quantum circuits is expensive for two distinct reasons. The obvious one is state-space size: an n-qubit system requires exponentially many amplitudes. The less obvious one is interference: useful output distributions emerge only after many computational histories have been coherently combined at common endpoints, and this aggregation step is itself a substantial source of cost. We introduce statistical interference sampling, a framework that makes this second bottleneck explicit by treating endpoint interference as a separately schedulable computation. Using the Chemical Abstract Machine (ChAM) as our model, weighted path contributions evolve as concurrent molecular species, and interference reactions combine contributions that share a common output state. A threshold rule terminates the process once an endpoint accumulates sufficient amplitude, discarding the remaining reactions. The method does not improve worst-case complexity and is not intended as a general-purpose simulator. Its purpose is to ask a more targeted question: how much of the interference calculation can be skipped while still recovering a useful output distribution? On benchmark circuits for Deutsch-Jozsa, Grover search, Simon's problem, and small Shor period-finding instances, we find that nearly 50% of endpoint interference reactions can be omitted while maintaining over 90% output accuracy for most algorithms tested. These results suggest that interference arithmetic is a structured resource that admits meaningful approximation, and that exposing it explicitly opens new opportunities for pruning strategies across path-sum, Pauli-path, and tensor-network simulation methods.

</details>


### [105] [An Explicit Scott-Type Bound for Absolutely Maximally Entangled States with Arbitrary Defect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01943)
*Shixuan Zeng,Xiande Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了关于有缺陷绝对最大纠缠（AME）态的Scott型上界的猜想，给出了任意缺陷l≥0的完全显式上界，这等价于给出了接近量子Singleton regime的一维纯量子纠错码的不存在性界。证明使用了截断MacWilliams线性规划系统和显式不可行性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Scott(2004)给出了AME态（k=⌊n/2⌋）的不存在性锐界；Ning等人研究了有缺陷的AME态（k=⌊n/2⌋-l, l>0），给出了l=1,2的显式Scott型界，并猜想了一般形式为(2l+2)q²+o(q²)的界。本文旨在解决该猜想。

Method: 使用截断MacWilliams线性规划系统（truncated MacWilliams linear-programming system）和显式不可行性证书（explicit infeasibility certificate）进行证明。

Result: 建立了任意缺陷l≥0的完全显式Scott型上界，统一了Scott的l=0界和Ning等人的l=1,2界；推导了固定局部维度q下k/n的显式渐近上界，改进了Ning等人的隐式上界。

Conclusion: 该结果不仅解决了Ning等人的猜想，还为量子秘密共享、量子掩蔽和量子纠错等领域提供了更精确的理论界限。

Abstract: Absolutely maximally entangled (AME) states and, more generally, $k$-uniform states in $(\C^q)^{\otimes n}$ are central objects in multipartite entanglement theory, with applications to quantum secret sharing, quantum masking, and quantum error correction. In the extremal case $k=\lfloor n/2\rfloor$, Scott (2004) proved a sharp nonexistence bound showing that AME states cannot exist once the number of parties $n$ exceeds a threshold of order $2q^{2}$ (with a parity dependence on $n$), where $q$ is the local dimension. Recently, Ning et al.\ studied \emph{defective} AME states (i.e., $k=\lfloor n/2\rfloor-l$ with $l>0$), gave explicit Scott-type bounds for defects $l=1,2$ and conjectured a general $(2l+2)q^{2}+o(q^{2})$ behavior. In this paper, we solve this conjecture and establish a fully explicit Scott-type upper bound for AME states with arbitrary defect $l\ge 0$, yielding Scott's bound for $l=0$ and Ning et al.'s bounds for $l=1,2$ as special cases. Equivalently, this gives nonexistence bounds for one-dimensional pure quantum error-correcting codes near the quantum Singleton regime. The proof uses a truncated MacWilliams linear-programming system and an explicit infeasibility certificate. As a direct application, we derive explicit asymptotic upper bounds on $k/n$ for fixed local dimension $q$, improving the implicit upper bounds given by Ning et al.

</details>


### [106] [Quantum secure blind decryption with two users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01949)
*Masahito Hayashi,Yuki Ito*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种量子安全盲解密协议，在保护用户文本隐私和服务器密钥隐私的前提下，实现用户2的解密。第二种协议额外保护密钥索引隐私，需要两个非对易服务器，并在后攻击场景下保持安全性，而经典协议无法满足这一要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现一种安全的盲解密机制，使用户（解密者）无法获知服务器的密钥，同时服务器也无法获知被解密的明文，并进一步探索量子协议在后攻击场景下相对于经典协议的安全性优势。

Method: 设计了两种量子安全盲解密协议：第一种实现基本的双向隐私保护；第二种引入非对易服务器以保护密钥索引隐私，并在协议完成后允许服务器间相互通信的场景下分析其安全性。

Result: 量子协议在后攻击场景下（两个服务器在协议完成后相互通信）仍能保持安全性，而经典协议在此类攻击下失败。

Conclusion: 量子盲解密协议在安全性和隐私保护方面优于经典协议，尤其是在服务器合谋的后攻击场景中具有显著优势。

Abstract: We propose two types of protocols for quantum secure blind decryption, involving two users and servers. User 1 holds the encrypted ciphertext. The servers store several indexed keys including the key encrypting the ciphertext. User 2 aims to obtain the decrypted text. The protocols are designed to preserve the following types of secrecy: Users ensure the secrecy of the text from the servers. Servers maintain the secrecy of the keys from the users. Our protocols enable User 2 to obtain the decrypted text while preserving these secrecy requirements. Additionally, the second protocol ensures the secrecy of the key index to identify the key encrypting the ciphertext from the servers, and the second protocol requires two non-commuting servers. Furthermore, we analyze the secrecy of the second protocol under post-attack scenarios, where the two servers communicates with each other after the completion of the protocol. We show that our quantum protocol satisfies the secrecy under these attacks, whereas its classical counterpart fails to do so.

</details>


### [107] [Quantum resonance encryption for secure data storage and communication with quantum kicked top](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01953)
*Sreeram PG,M. S. Santhanam*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于量子 kicked top 动力学系统的量子协议，用于保护共享量子计算机中用户数据的隐私，即使服务提供者也无法访问用户数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共享量子计算环境中，确保用户数据隐私并防止未授权方（包括服务提供商）访问的问题。

Method: 基于量子 kicked top（自旋系统动力学）在量子共振区间的运行，实现授权用户的完美恢复、窃听者拦截态呈现混合态，并内置篡改检测机制。

Result: 该协议可确保授权用户完美恢复数据，使窃听者只能获得混合态，并具备篡改检测能力；同时可扩展到安全通信和量子密钥分发。

Conclusion: 在配备量子存储和量子网络的量子计算机上有效，目前也可在现有量子计算平台上进行实验室验证。

Abstract: In a shared quantum computer, how to ensure data privacy and protection from access by unauthorized parties? We propose a genuine quantum protocol for protecting user's data which is not accessible even to the service provider. The protocol is based on quantum kicked top -- the dynamics of a spin system --operating at quantum resonance regime. This protocol ensures perfect recovery for authorized users while making intercepted states appear mixed to eavesdroppers, with built-in tampering detection. This protocol can also be used for secure communication between two parties in geographically different locations, and also for quantum key distribution. The effectiveness of this protocol is demonstrated by assuming a quantum computer with quantum memory and functioning quantum networks. In the absence of the latter, at present, the protocol can be demonstrated in laboratory using currently available quantum computing platforms.

</details>


### [108] [Lie Algebra-Based Quantum Optimal Controls Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02014)
*Piero Luchi,Francesco Pederiva*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合李群理论与前馈神经网络的框架，用于高效生成超导量子比特系统中任意幺正操作的量子最优控制脉冲，避免了推理时的显式优化。该方法在2、3、4量子比特系统上实现了高保真度重构，并成功泛化至中微子集体味振荡的Trotter传播子，展示了单个模型可作为任意兼容希尔伯特空间维度的通用控制脉冲生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准量子最优控制方法在处理大量不同传播子时面临计算瓶颈，因希尔伯特空间维度随量子比特数指数增长，尤其是在Trotter化量子模拟场景中，需为每个目标传播子单独执行优化，计算成本极高。

Method: 通过李群理论预计算一组代表性控制脉冲，并训练前馈神经网络学习从目标传播子到对应控制脉冲的映射关系，从而在推理阶段直接生成脉冲，无需显式优化。

Result: 在2、3、4量子比特的超导系统上实现高重构保真度，并在中微子集体味振荡的Trotter传播子任务上验证了泛化能力。

Conclusion: 单个模型在硬件特定随机传播子上训练后，可作为任意兼容希尔伯特空间维度目标系统的通用控制脉冲生成器，为可扩展量子模拟提供新路径。

Abstract: We present a framework combining Lie group theory and feed-forward neural networks to efficiently generate quantum optimal control pulses for arbitrary unitary operations in superconducting qubit systems, bypassing the need for explicit optimization at inference time. The exponential scaling of the Hilbert space dimension with qubit number makes standard optimization approaches computationally prohibitive when large ensembles of distinct propagators must be processed, a bottleneck that is particularly acute in Trotterized quantum simulation. Our method addresses this limitation by pre-computing a representative set of control pulses via Lie group theory and training neural networks to map target propagators to their corresponding pulses. We demonstrate the approach on superconducting qubit systems of 2, 3, and 4 qubits, finding high reconstruction fidelity for specific combinations of Lie algebra parameters. As a physically motivated benchmark, we apply the methodology to reconstruct control pulses for the Trotter propagators of a neutrino system undergoing collective flavor oscillations. The successful generalization across system types demonstrates that a single model -- trained once on hardware-specific random propagators -- can serve as a universal control-pulse generator for any target quantum system of compatible Hilbert space dimension, offering a promising route toward scalable quantum simulation.

</details>


### [109] [Branch-Aware Quantum Constant Propagation for Dynamic Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02018)
*Innocenzo Fulginiti,Yanbin Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出分支感知量子常量传播（BQCP），一种针对动态量子电路（含中途测量和经典前馈）的编译时分析方法。BQCP通过跟踪不同执行分支上的经典信息和量子状态，实现比现有方法更精确的路径敏感优化，并证明了分析的正确性和简化的保义性。实验表明，在动态电路上BQCP的缩减效果优于包括QCP在内的现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子编译优化方法大多仅针对酉电路（unitary circuits），不支持包含中途测量和经典前馈的动态电路（dynamic circuits），而动态电路在近期含噪硬件上越来越重要，亟需能够处理这类电路的编译时分析和优化方法。

Method: BQCP扩展了量子常量传播（QCP），通过追踪中途测量产生的经典信息以及对应的测量后量子态，并跨不同执行分支进行路径敏感推理。为保持可扩展性，对量子态表示大小和跟踪分支数均设置上限边界。利用分析推断的信息，对电路操作进行语义保持的化简。

Result: 在应用驱动和合成基准测试上，BQCP在动态电路上的缩减效果一致优于包括QCP在内的现有优化方法。

Conclusion: BQCP填补了动态量子电路编译时优化的空白，通过路径敏感的分支感知分析实现了更精确的信息传播和更大的电路简化，且方法具有可扩展性和理论正确性保证。

Abstract: Compile-time optimization is important for improving the efficiency and reliability of quantum circuits on current noisy hardware. While many existing methods simplify circuits using structural patterns or quantum-state information, most of them target only unitary circuits and do not support dynamic circuits with mid-circuit measurements and classical feedforward. In this work, we present Branch-Aware Quantum Constant Propagation (BQCP), a compile-time analysis for dynamic circuits. BQCP extends Quantum Constant Propagation (QCP) by tracking the classical information produced by mid-circuit measurements together with the corresponding post-measurement quantum states across different execution branches. This enables path-sensitive reasoning inside conditional blocks and more precise information propagation than QCP. To keep the analysis scalable, we bound both the size of the quantum-state representation and the number of tracked branches. Using the information inferred by the analysis, we apply semantics-preserving simplifications to circuit operations. We prove the soundness of both the analysis and the simplifications. Experimental results on both application-driven and synthetic benchmarks show that, on dynamic circuits, our method consistently achieves larger reductions than other existing passes including QCP.

</details>


### [110] [Penalty-free quantum optimization applied to lattice protein folding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02104)
*Leif Gellsersen,Anders Irbäck,Lucas Knuthson,Stefan Prestel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于QAOA的变体方法，通过将格点蛋白质折叠问题映射为冲突图上的最大独立集问题，避免了二次惩罚项的使用，并通过经典模拟（N=4,6）和启发式迭代局部搜索（N≤14）验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 格点蛋白质的最小能量结构识别是一个离散优化难题。传统的量子方法（如量子退火和QAOA）在将问题映射为二进制表示时，通常需要引入二次惩罚项来强制有效的链构型，这增加了问题的复杂性和实现难度。本文旨在通过将搜索空间限制为冲突图中的独立集，避免使用二次惩罚项，从而简化优化过程。

Method: 论文采用了一种专为最大独立集问题设计的QAOA混合器，将格点蛋白质折叠问题映射到冲突图上。目标函数仅包含蛋白质能量和一个简单的线性偏置项，无需二次惩罚。对于小规模系统（N=4,6），通过经典模拟量子电路进行验证；对于更大系统（N≤14），引入了一种启发式迭代局部搜索方案，使用最多26个量子比特的局部子图进行折叠。

Result: 经典模拟验证了该方法对N=4和N=6格点蛋白质的有效性。通过启发式迭代局部搜索，成功完成了长度达N=14的格点蛋白质折叠，且使用的局部子图最多仅需26个量子比特。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了通过最大独立集映射和专用QAOA混合器，可以在不使用二次惩罚项的情况下有效求解格点蛋白质折叠问题，并通过局部搜索策略将方法扩展到更大规模系统，为量子计算在生物分子结构预测中的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Identifying minimum-energy structures of lattice proteins is a challenging discrete optimization problem. Quantum approaches such as analog quantum annealing and the gate-based quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) can address this problem after mapping it to a binary representation, which typically involves introducing penalty terms to enforce valid chain configurations. However, in this and many related problems, the use of quadratic penalty terms can be avoided by restricting the search space to independent sets in a conflict graph and using a QAOA mixer designed for the maximum independent set problem. In this work, we implement and explore this QAOA variant for lattice protein folding. Here, the objective function consists solely of the protein energy together with a simple linear bias term, without quadratic penalties. We validate this approach through classical simulations of the quantum circuits for lattice proteins of lengths $N=4$ and $N=6$. To explore larger systems, we further introduce a heuristic iterative local-search scheme, with which we successfully fold lattice proteins with lengths up to $N=14$ using local subgraphs with at most 26 qubits.

</details>


### [111] [Quantum Dynamics of a Particle in a Linear Potential: Invariant Operator Approach and Discrete Spectrum Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02112)
*Mustapha Maamache,Aymen Bendjoudl*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用Lewis-Riesenfeld不变量算子方法，研究了线性势中粒子的量子动力学，通过构建厄米二次不变量并借助幺正变换将其简化为谐振子哈密顿量形式，获得了精确的量子描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在利用Lewis-Riesenfeld不变量算子方法精确描述恒定外力作用下粒子的量子动力学行为，并建立不变量理论与谐振子量子化之间的直接联系。

Method: 从含时薛定谔方程出发，构造最一般的厄米二次不变量，导出其含时系数的耦合微分方程组；通过一系列幺正变换将不变量算子约化为谐振子哈密顿量形式；根据守恒量ω²的符号对系统进行分类，重点关注ω²>0的离散本征谱情况。

Result: 获得了不变量系数、位移参数和变换后波函数的明确解析表达式，建立了适用于恒定外力作用下粒子的精确量子描述框架。

Conclusion: 该 formalism 成功实现了对线性势中粒子量子动力学的精确求解，并通过与谐振子量子化的联系，为处理此类问题提供了系统化的不变量方法途径。

Abstract: We investigate the quantum dynamics of a particle subjected to a linear potential using the Lewis--Riesenfeld invariant operator method. Starting from the time-dependent Schrödinger equation associated with a constant external force, we construct the most general Hermitian quadratic invariant and derive the corresponding coupled differential equations for its time-dependent coefficients. By means of an appropriate sequence of unitary transformations, the invariant operator is reduced to the form of a harmonic oscillator Hamiltonian. This reduction enables a clear classification of the system according to the sign of the conserved quantity ω2. Particular attention is devoted to the physically relevant case ω2 >0, which yields a discrete eigenspectrum. Explicit analytical expressions for the invariant coefficients, the displacement parameters, and the transformed wave functions are obtained. The resulting formalism provides an exact quantum description of a particle under a constant force and establishes a direct connection between invariant theory and harmonic oscillator quantization.

</details>


### [112] [Can scrambling protect quantum state distinguishability under noise?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02122)
*Guoding Liu,Chushi Qin,Zitai Xu,Xiongfeng Ma,Zi-Wen Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将量子态区分性从两态扩展到系综，研究了2-设计系综在噪声下的区分性。通过解耦方法，建立了有噪系综区分性的紧界，发现存在由信道条件熵控制的锐利阈值和相变行为：低于阈值时状态仍可区分，高于阈值时区分性先幂律衰减后指数坍塌。但后测量系综在任何噪声下都指数不可区分，揭示了未测量与后测量加扰系综的根本差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态区分性是量子信息科学的基础概念，但传统上仅针对两态对。实际量子信息处理中噪声不可避免，而信息加扰（scrambling）被认为可能保护量子信息。本文旨在探索最小加扰的2-设计系综能否在噪声下保持可区分性，揭示噪声与信息加扰之间的竞争关系。

Method: 采用严谨的解耦方法（decoupling approach）建立有噪系综区分性的紧界。通过平均迹距离刻画系综区分性，分析2-设计系综在噪声下的行为，并对比未测量与后测量两种场景。

Result: 1) 未测量的有噪2-设计系综区分性存在锐利阈值和相变：低于信道条件熵阈值时高概率可区分，高于阈值时先幂律衰减后指数坍塌；2) 后测量的有噪2-设计系综在任何噪声下都指数不可区分，不存在保护窗口；3) 局部纯度收缩噪声下，后测量系综对所有测量都指数不可区分。

Conclusion: 揭示了未测量与后测量加扰系综在噪声下的根本差异：前者在足够小噪声下可保持高区分性，后者则无任何保护机制。该结果对量子通信、密码学和阴影断层扫描等学习任务有重要启示。

Abstract: Quantum state distinguishability is a fundamental concept in quantum information science that underpins a wide range of important practical tasks. Traditionally formulated for pairs of states, quantum state distinguishability is here extended to quantum state ensembles, which we characterize through the average pairwise trace distance. Motivated by both theoretical and practical interest in noisy quantum information processing, we ask whether ``minimally'' scrambled ensembles modeled by 2-designs protect distinguishability under noise, which sheds light on the fundamental competition between noise and information scrambling. Using a rigorous decoupling approach, we establish tight bounds on noisy ensemble distinguishability. We show that the distinguishability of noisy 2-design ensembles exhibits a sharp threshold and phase-transition behavior governed by channel conditional entropy: below the threshold, the states remain mutually distinguishable with high probability, while above it, distinguishability undergoes a sudden power-law decay and then collapses exponentially. On the other hand, under local purity-shrinking noise, post-measured noisy 2-design ensembles become exponentially indistinguishable for any measurement, precluding a noise threshold for learning tasks such as shadow tomography. These results reveal a sharp difference between unmeasured and post-measured scrambled ensembles: the former can retain high distinguishability for sufficiently small noise, whereas the latter exhibits no such protected regime. We discuss the implications of these results for crucial tasks ranging from quantum communication and cryptography to learning.

</details>


### [113] [Spin Hamiltonian as Matrix-Free Linear Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02169)
*Aditya Dev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需显式矩阵组装的通用自旋哈密顿量作用算法，通过混合进制索引实现张量积基下的状态更新，支持任意自旋格点和混合自旋系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统精确对角化方法中显式组装哈密顿量矩阵导致的内存消耗大、计算效率低的问题，同时弥合基索引逻辑与无矩阵状态更新框架之间的鸿沟。

Method: 采用混合进制索引对全张量积基进行编码，将局部自旋操作映射为简单的整数偏移量，推导出单/双位点项在任意自旋格点（含混合自旋系统）上的闭式求值框架。

Result: 实现了单一均匀循环适用于所有位点（不论局域希尔伯特空间维度），算法可并行化、内存高效，并支持限制基或截断玻色子能级扩展。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了精确对角化索引逻辑与无矩阵更新范式的统一框架，为大规模自旋系统的数值模拟提供了高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: We present an algorithm that computes the action of a generic spin Hamiltonian on a state vector on the fly, entirely avoiding explicit matrix assembly. This is achieved through mixed-radix indexing of the full tensor-product basis, which translates local spin operations into simple integer offsets. The result is an explicit framework for evaluating single- and two-site terms across arbitrary spin lattices, including mixed-spin systems. Our construction bridges the basis-indexing logic familiar from exact diagonalization with the matrix-free state-update philosophy of address-based frameworks. By writing the indexing logic in closed form, a single uniform loop applies to every site regardless of its local Hilbert-space dimension. The method is parallelizable and memory-conserving, and can be extended to restricted basis or truncated bosonic levels.

</details>


### [114] [Quantum-inspired Topographic Stereovision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02197)
*Fanglin Bao,Youfei Xie*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文挑战了远距离立体视觉中传统的三角测量方法，提出距离梯度而非绝对距离才是相关可观测量。通过信息遗憾分析发现绝对距离和距离梯度的最优测量不同且不兼容，为此引入立体正则化和地形干涉仪，实现了超越瑞利极限的热源地形立体视觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战远距离立体视觉中长期未被质疑的三角测量范式，指出在远距离场景中形状（地形）而非绝对距离才是相关可观测量，现有测量方案存在可观测量-测量不匹配问题。

Method: 1) 信息遗憾分析揭示绝对距离与距离梯度的最优测量不兼容；2) 提出立体正则化方法解决违反发射体数量守恒的立体各向异性；3) 提出地形干涉仪，将视差路径转化为马赫-曾德尔干涉仪两臂，引入中心路径作为本地振荡器实现平衡零差检测，饱和量子Fisher信息。

Result: 实现了改进的地形误差标度，超越瑞利极限的热源地形立体视觉，建立了量子启发的热辅助探测与测距框架。

Conclusion: 该工作为遥感天文学中的热源探测提供了量子启发的理论框架，推动了远距离立体视觉向地形测量范式的转变。

Abstract: We challenge the long-unquestioned triangulation in distant stereovision, where shape rather than distance is the relevant observable. Our information-regret analysis reveals that the optimal measurements for absolute distance and distance gradient are unexpectedly different and incompatible. To resolve this observable-measurement mismatch, we introduce stereo regularization to address stereo anisotropies that violate prevailing emitter-number conservation, and propose the topographic interferometer, which exploits cross-detector correlations to probe topography without measuring the distance profile. Our interferometer turns parallaxing paths into Mach-Zehnder arms and incorporates a central path as the local oscillator for balanced homodyne detection, saturating the quantum Fisher information with improved topographic error scaling. Our work enables topographic stereovision of thermal sources beyond the Rayleigh limit, thereby establishing a quantum-inspired framework for heat-assisted detection and ranging in remote sensing and astronomy.

</details>


### [115] [Iterative $C_Z$-gate-based protocol for squeezed Schrödinger cat state engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02201)
*Roman Goncharov,N. G. Veselkova,Alexei D. Kiselev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于测量辅助门的方案，用于生成和操控压缩薛定谔猫态，通过非高斯小振幅辅助态与目标振荡器进行QND纠缠操作后执行零差测量，可实现高保真度、可调尺寸和压缩度的猫态生成，并引入迭代式C_Z协议用于猫态放大，适用于测量型量子计算和混合量子网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩光学薛定谔猫态是量子理论基础检验和量子技术的关键资源，现有生成和操控方法在保真度、尺寸可调性和成功率方面存在局限，需要更高效的方案。

Method: 提出测量辅助门方案：将非高斯小振幅（一般压缩）薛定谔猫态作为辅助态，与初始制备在压缩真空（或相干）态的目标振荡器进行QND纠缠操作，随后执行投影零差测量。还引入基于零差条件作用的迭代C_Z协议用于猫态放大。

Result: 该门能够生成高保真度的压缩薛定谔猫态，且猫态尺寸和压缩度可调，保真度与成功概率之间存在可调节的权衡关系。分析了实现所需保真度和成功概率的参数区间。

Conclusion: 该方案适用于测量型量子计算和混合量子网络，其中非高斯资源可增强计算和通信能力。

Abstract: Squeezed optical Schrödinger cat states constitute a key resource for both fundamental tests of quantum theory and up-to-date quantum technologies. We propose a measurement-assisted gate for the generation and manipulation of the cat states. In this scheme, an ancilla in the non-Gaussian small-amplitude (in general, squeezed) Schrödinger cat state and the target oscillator initially prepared in a squeezed vacuum (or coherent) state are subjected to a quantum nondemolition (QND) entangling operation followed by projective homodyne measurement. The proposed gate enables generation of high-fidelity squeezed Schrödinger cat states with controllable size and squeezing with tunable fidelity/success-probability trade-off. We also introduce an iterative, homodyne-conditioned $C_Z$-based protocol for cat-state amplification. The parameter regimes required to achieve the desired fidelity and the success probability are analyzed. The approach is well suited for applications in measurement-based quantum computing and hybrid quantum networks where non-Gaussian resources enhance computational and communication capabilities.

</details>


### [116] [Optimized Point Addition Circuits for Elliptic Curve Discrete Logarithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02235)
*André Schrottenloher*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对Babbush等人（arXiv 2026）提出的椭圆曲线离散对数量子计算优化方案，公开了详细的逻辑量子电路架构。在secp256k1曲线上，相比Babbush等人的结果，本方案的量子比特数仅增加约1.5%，而Toffoli门数量减少6.5%至10%。同时提供了适用于任意素域的通用电路门数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子攻击研究（如Shor算法）中，椭圆曲线离散对数的量子计算成本虽有改进（Babbush et al. 2026），但Babbush等人未公开其逻辑量子电路，仅依赖零知识证明。本文旨在公开详细的逻辑电路架构，验证并改进该结果。

Method: 基于优化的椭圆曲线素域上的点加法电路，设计详细的逻辑量子电路架构。针对secp256k1曲线进行特异性优化，并给出适用于任意素域的通用电路变体。

Result: 与Babbush等人相比：量子比特数增加约1.5%，Toffoli门数减少6.5%至10%（secp256k1曲线）。同时提供通用素域电路的门数估计。

Conclusion: 本文公开了与Babbush等人性能相近甚至更优的逻辑量子电路，增强了量子攻击成本评估的透明性和可复现性，为进一步优化提供了基准。

Abstract: Shor's algorithm represents the main threat of quantum computers to cryptography. In order to precisely understand its feasibility, many authors have worked towards reducing its costs, either at the logical level (assuming a fault-tolerant architecture), or at the physical level (taking into account the constraints of envisioned hardware). In particular, recent works by Chevignard et al. (CRYPTO 2024) and Gidney (arXiv 2025) used improved arithmetic to significantly reduce the qubit cost of factoring RSA public keys.
  Even more recently, Babbush et al. (arXiv 2026) improved the cost of computing elliptic curve discrete logarithms, with a reduction of a factor 2 to 3 in gate count and qubit count compared to a previous work by Litinski (arXiv 2023). Their result relies on optimized point addition circuits on elliptic curves over prime fields. However they did not reveal their logical quantum circuits, relying instead on a zero-knowledge proof.
  In this paper, we detail a quantum logical circuit architecture which gives similar results as Babbush et al., with a slightly higher number of qubits (around 1.5% increase) and a slightly smaller Toffoli gate count (between 6.5% and 10% reduction) for the curve secp256k1. We also give gate counts for a generic variant of the circuit, which is valid for any prime field.

</details>


### [117] [Quantum optimal control of the Dicke manifold in Rydberg atom arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02283)
*Ivy Pannier-Günther,Vikas Buchemmavari,Pablo M. Poggi,Ivan H. Deutsch*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出“不可约表示蒸馏”（IRD）方法，结合量子最优控制（GrAPE），在仅需线性缩放希尔伯特空间维度下，有效抑制Rydberg原子阵列中偶极-偶极相互作用导致的对称子空间泄漏，实现了Dicke态、GHZ态及极值量子态的高保真制备，并系统评估了量子速度极限与保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体系统的态制备与控制面临希尔伯特空间指数增长的挑战，而Rydberg原子阵列中偶极相互作用的有限范围导致对称子空间（Dicke流形）发生泄漏，现有方法难以同时兼顾可扩展性与保真度。

Method: 提出“不可约蒸馏”（IRD）方法，将对称子空间耦合到泄漏误差空间的过程用线性规模希尔伯特维度捕获；结合梯度上升脉冲工程（GrAPE），在无或少量局域寻址的控制方案下优化脉冲，生成GHZ态、Dicke态和极值量子态。

Result: 对所提IRD-GrAPE方案进行了量子速度极限（QSL）基准测试；在小系统上精确测试了脉冲保真度，并在更大系统上利用高阶IRD预测了保真度。

Conclusion: IRD-GrAPE方法在Rydberg原子阵列平台上有效抑制了对称子空间泄漏，实现了可扩展的量子态制备，为多体量子控制提供了新途径。

Abstract: The ability to engineer and control quantum states of many-body systems is a central challenge in quantum information science. For a register of $N$ qubits, the full Hilbert space dimension grows exponentially as $2^N$, rendering generic state preparation and control infeasible without exploiting structure or symmetry. A particularly important and physically motivated restriction is to the fully symmetric subspace, spanned by the Dicke states, which are simultaneous eigenstates of collective spin $J=N/2$. Ensembles of Rydberg atoms interacting via electric dipoles in two-dimensional tweezer arrays form a promising platform for achieving such control. However, the finite range of dipole-dipole interactions poses a challenge to generating and controlling the Dicke manifold because the Hamiltonian incurs leakage from the computational subspace. To counteract this leakage, we perform quantum optimal control algorithms on a truncated Hilbert space according to our newly developed method of ``irrep distillation'' (IRD), which captures the process by which the symmetric subspace couples to leakage error-spaces, using only linear-scaling Hilbert dimension. We implement gradient ascent pulse engineering (GrAPE) on control schemes with little or no local addressing, to generate resourceful states like Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger, Dicke, and extremal quantum states. We benchmark each scheme of IRD-GrAPE for its quantum speed limit (QSL), as well as exactly testing pulse fidelities on small system sizes and predicting fidelities using higher-order IRD on larger systems.

</details>


### [118] [Hidden $\mathfrak{u}(2,1)$ symmetry and Jordan chains in a resonant ghostly three-dimensional model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02290)
*Andreas Fring,Ian Marquette*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究三维鬼魅哈密顿量实现下的完全简并共振六阶Pais-Uhlenbeck振子，揭示了经典相流中非对角化的复共轭Jordan链结构，以及在量子化后由交织算子生成的隐藏u(2,1)代数及其有限维不变子空间中的Jordan结构；进一步导出了基于李点对称的三哈密顿量形式，并分析了其中心化子与可积性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索共振高阶导数系统中隐藏对称性、经典与量子Jordan结构以及多哈密顿量几何之间的共存关系，作为非共振情形的对比补充。

Method: 采用三维鬼魅哈密顿量实现完全简并共振六阶Pais-Uhlenbeck振子；通过量子化构建交织算子生成隐藏u(2,1)代数；利用李点对称导出三哈密顿量形式；分析中心化子U(u(2,1))中高阶候选算子的可约性。

Result: 经典相流表现为非对角化的两个复共轭长度为3的Jordan链；量子化后得到有限维不变子空间承载Jordan结构，且其sl₂子模分解与哈密顿量的Jordan分解一般不一致；三哈密顿量形式由隐藏代数编码，但不存在正定线性组合生成相同动力学；中心化子中的高阶候选Q可约，不产生独立守恒量。

Conclusion: 该模型是首个同时展现隐藏u(2,1)对称性、经典与量子Jordan结构以及多哈密顿量几何的共振高阶导数系统，揭示了共振情形下与非共振情形的重要差异。

Abstract: We investigate a three-dimensional ghostly Hamiltonian realisation of the fully degenerate resonant sixth-order Pais-Uhlenbeck oscillator. On the classical level, the phase-space flow is non-diagonalisable and decomposes into two complex-conjugate Jordan chains of length three, explaining the appearance of oscillatory solutions with secular terms.
  Upon quantisation, we construct intertwining operators whose quadratic combinations generate a hidden spectrum-generating $\mathfrak{u}(2,1)$-algebra. The associated descendant spaces are finite-dimensional invariant subspaces carrying non-trivial Jordan structure. Although these spaces admit a natural decomposition into irreducible modules of a distinguished $\mathfrak{sl}_2$-subalgebra, this decomposition does not in general coincide with the Jordan decomposition of the Hamiltonian.
  We further derive a tri-Hamiltonian formulation from Lie point symmetries of the classical flow and show that the corresponding Hamiltonians are naturally encoded by the same hidden algebra. Nevertheless, unlike in the non-resonant case, no positive-definite linear combination of them generates the same dynamics. Finally, we analyse the common centraliser of the tri-Hamiltonian family in $U(\mathfrak u(2,1))$, showing that the natural higher-order candidate $Q$ is reducible and yields no independent classical or quantum integral.
  The model thus provides a resonant higher-derivative system in which hidden $\mathfrak{u}(2,1)$ symmetry, classical and quantum Jordan structures, and multi-Hamiltonian geometry coexist.

</details>


### [119] [Is the most random pattern random? Maximizing localization in a two-dimensional lattice with engineered disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02291)
*Morgan Berkane,Sahel Ashhab*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在二维正方晶格（紧束缚模型和量子比特晶格）中，通过精心选择参数（如原位能）而非随机分布来最大化局域化量化参数。数值计算表明工程化局域化显著超过随机分布的平均局域化。结合微扰论改进代价函数，并将单粒子模型结果迁移至量子比特晶格以实现最大退耦，可用于优化量子处理器的空闲态设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统安德森局域化依赖于随机参数分布，本文探索能否通过确定性参数选取（而非随机性）实现更强的局域化效果，并将这一思想从单粒子系统推广到量子比特晶格系统。

Method: 数值优化局域化量化参数；微扰论分析局域化机制并改进代价函数；将单粒子紧束缚模型的最优参数设置迁移到小规模量子比特晶格系统中。

Result: 工程化确定的参数设置产生的局域化显著超过随机分布的平均局域化；改进后的代价函数在单粒子和全希尔伯特空间中均增强了局域化；单粒子模型的最优参数可适配用于量子比特晶格系统实现最大退耦。

Conclusion: 通过确定性参数设计可以超越随机安德森局域化的局域化强度；单粒子紧束缚模型的研究结果可为量子处理器空闲态参数优化提供指导。

Abstract: We investigate localization in two models: a single particle in a two-dimensional square lattice described by the tight binding Hamiltonian, and a two-dimensional square qubit lattice. It is well-known that Anderson localization occurs under suitable conditions in which the system parameters are chosen randomly from some statistical distribution. We propose a situation in which the parameters, specifically the on-site energies, are carefully chosen in such a way that a localization-quantifying parameter is maximized. We demonstrate the optimization procedure with numerical calculations in which the engineered localization significantly exceeds the average localization caused by a random distribution of the on-site energies. We explore the relation between spatial patterns and localization efficiency. Furthermore, we use perturbation theory to gain insight into the localization mechanism and obtain an improved cost function for optimization calculations, leading to enhanced localization in both the single-particle and full Hilbert spaces. Although large-scale simulations for qubit lattices are computationally infeasible, we use small-system simulations to demonstrate that results obtained using the single-particle tight binding model can be adapted to identify optimal settings for qubit lattice systems to achieve maximum decoupling between the qubits, which can be valuable for optimizing the idle-state settings on a quantum processor.

</details>


### [120] [Defect Holonomy Near Rank-Deficient Mixed States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02343)
*Yu-Huan Huang,Xu-Yang Hou,Hao Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合量子态在秩变化点附近的几何结构，发现这些奇点可作为有效几何缺陷。通过分析Uhlmann联络在满秩密度矩阵流形上的定义与秩缺陷边界层的奇异性，证明在剔除奇异集的 punctured 流形上存在良好定义的规范结构，并识别出围绕缺陷的非平凡环路所携带的Uhlmann holonomy作为渐近鲁棒不变量。在精确可解的qutrit模型中，存在局部平坦但具有非平凡单值的子流形，类似Aharonov-Bohm型输运。Holonomy仅依赖于消失特征值的比值和固定角向环路，而Uhlmann曲率则以路径依赖的方式发散。该渐近holonomy由punctured流形的拓扑保护，对环路和平滑变形不敏感，给出围绕秩缺陷的连续非量子化分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子态的几何相（Uhlmann相）在接近秩变化点时面临定义困难，因为Uhlmann联络仅在满秩密度矩阵上有良好定义。理解这些奇点附近的几何行为，以及是否存在鲁棒的拓扑不变量，是量子几何相理论中的重要问题。

Method: 通过构建punctured密度矩阵流形（剔除秩缺陷奇异集），在满秩区域上定义Uhlmann联络；利用精确可解的qutrit模型构造具有非平凡单值的局域平坦联络；分析特征值收缩过程中holonomy的渐近行为与曲率的发散规律。

Result: Uhlmann holonomy在特征值趋于零时保持有限且鲁棒，仅依赖于消失特征值的比值（光谱前因子）和固定角向环路，而对径向分布不敏感；Uhlmann曲率则以路径依赖方式发散，其主导项遵循光谱前因子的标度律。在有效SU(2)缺陷扇区中，holonomy的共轭类（等价于Wilson圈变量）提供了围绕秩缺陷的连续非量子化渐近单值分类。

Conclusion: 即使Uhlmann曲率发散且非普适，Uhlmann holonomy仍然作为普适渐近不变量存在，其鲁棒性由punctured流形的拓扑保护，不受环路光滑变形或径向轮廓影响。这为混合量子态在奇点附近的几何相提供了新的拓扑理解框架。

Abstract: We investigate the geometry of mixed quantum states near rank-changing points, showing that these singularities function as effective geometric defects. The Uhlmann connection is well-defined only on the full-rank sector of the density-matrix manifold, while rank-deficient states form singular boundary strata where the bundle structure degenerates. By restricting to a punctured state manifold that excludes the singular set, we obtain a well-defined gauge structure and identify an asymptotically robust invariant: the Uhlmann holonomy around noncontractible loops encircling the defect. In an exactly solvable qutrit model, a restricted submanifold emerges on which the connection is locally flat yet carries nontrivial monodromy, analogous to flat connections with Aharonov--Bohm-type transport. The holonomy depends only on the ratios of the vanishing eigenvalues under frozen radial dependence of the eigenbasis geometry and a fixed angular loop. In contrast, the Uhlmann curvature may diverge path-dependently when eigenvalues shrink with distinct powers, with a leading spectral-prefactor scaling law, establishing that the holonomy survives as a universal asymptotic invariant while the curvature remains non-universal. Within the effective SU(2) defect sector, the conjugacy class of the holonomy, equivalently the Wilson loop variable, provides a continuous, non-quantized classification of the asymptotic monodromy surrounding the rank-deficient defect. This non-quantization does not imply a lack of robustness: the asymptotic holonomy is protected by the topology of the punctured manifold and is insensitive to smooth deformations of the loop or the radial profile.

</details>


### [121] [Optical Stability and Photophysics of NV Centers in Diamond up to 120 GPa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02399)
*Kin On Ho,Cassandra Dailledouze,Vytautas Žalandauskas,Grégoire Le Caruyer,Marek Maciaszek,Claire Roussy,Marie-Pierre Adam,Martin Schmidt,Loïc Toraille,Paul Loubeyre,Lukas Razinkovas,Jean-François Roch*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过实验和理论研究，系统分析了NV色心在高达~120 GPa静水压力下的光学性质演化，包括零声子线位置、辐射寿命、光学线形和光电离阈值，验证了NV色心在极端高压下作为量子传感器的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NV色心在高压研究中已成为重要的量子传感器，但其某些物理特性仍需进一步研究，以优化基于NV色心的高压传感性能。

Method: 采用实验与理论相结合的方法，研究NV色心在高达120 GPa静水压力下的零声子线位置、辐射寿命、光学线形和光电离阈值等光学性质的演化规律。

Result: 系统揭示了NV色心在高压下各光学参数的变化趋势，并提供了高压光学实验的光谱学指导原则。

Conclusion: NV色心在极端静水压力下仍是一种稳健的量子传感器，尤其适用于磁学表征应用。

Abstract: The nitrogen vacancy (NV) center has emerged as a powerful quantum sensor in high-pressure research, with the observation of optically detected magnetic resonance at megabar pressures. However, some aspects of NV physics require further investigation to optimize the development of NV-based sensing under pressure. Here, we study both experimentally and theoretically the optical properties of the NV center under hydrostatic pressure. We investigate the evolution of the zero-phonon line (ZPL) position, radiative lifetimes, optical lineshapes, and photoionization thresholds of the NV center under pressures up to ~120 GPa. We also provide spectroscopic guidelines for performing high-pressure optical experiments. Our results confirm that the NV center remains a robust quantum sensor under extreme hydrostatic pressures, especially for magnetic characterization.

</details>


### [122] [Bounds on Nonlocality and Random Access Codes from Extended Information Causality Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02416)
*Prabhav Jain,Nikolai Miklin,Mariami Gachechiladze*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩展信息因果性原理对非局域关联的约束，推导了新的量子贝尔不等式族，改进了Collins-Gisin族的不等式界限，并分析了纠缠辅助随机接入码的理论界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息因果性作为约束非局域关联的物理原理，其原始形式对某些场景约束不够紧。通过扩展该原理以允许Alice输入之间的关联，可以获得更严格的约束，从而更精确地刻画量子关联边界。

Method: 将信息因果性扩展到允许Alice输入之间存在关联的场景，推导量子贝尔不等式族，应用于二元输入输出之外的更一般场景，并分析纠缠辅助随机接入码的获胜概率界限。

Result: 获得了比原始信息因果性更紧的约束，推导了新的量子贝尔不等式族，改进了Collins-Gisin族不等式的解析界限，并为纠缠辅助随机接入码导出了新的理论无关解析界限。

Conclusion: 扩展信息因果性在一般场景下比原始形式更强，但对纠缠辅助随机接入码类问题，扩展原理未能改进原始信息因果性给出的界限，表明原始界限对该类问题已是最优。

Abstract: Information Causality was introduced as a physical principle for constraining the set of nonlocal correlations. In recent work, we proposed an extension of Information Causality that allows correlations among Alice's inputs. This extended principle yields tighter constraints than the original formulation and recovers part of the quantum boundary in certain Bell scenarios. In this work, we further investigate the implications of extended Information Causality and apply it to scenarios beyond binary inputs and outputs. We derive a family of quantum Bell inequalities that strengthen previously known constraints on quantum correlations. Using these inequalities, we obtain an improved analytical bound for the Collins-Gisin family of Bell inequalities. We also apply Information Causality to entanglement-assisted random access codes and derive new theory-independent analytical bounds on the winning probability. For this latter task, we prove that, despite being stronger in general, the extended principle does not improve the bounds obtained from the original Information Causality principle. This suggests that the existing Information Causality bounds are optimal for this class of random access codes.

</details>


### [123] [Evolutionary Discovery of Bivariate Bicycle Codes with LLM-Guided Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02418)
*Juan Cruz-Benito,Andrew W. Cross,David Kremer,Ismael Faro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种LLM引导的进化工作流，通过语言模型变异生成双变量自行车码和扰动双变量自行车码的Python程序，在约1650次进化迭代中筛选了约2×10^5个候选码，耗时约140小时、花费约400美元。在码长n≤360时发现了465个不同候选码，包括CSS和非CSS变体，其中包含[[288,16,12]]不可分解码和[[144,12,12]]等高性能码，表明LLM引导的程序进化可作为一种实用的结构化量子码发现工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子LDPC码的发现需要搜索庞大的代数设计空间，并可靠地认证任何候选码的参数和等价类，传统方法效率有限。因此，本文引入LLM引导的进化工作流来探索这一搜索空间。

Method: 采用LLM引导的进化工作流，语言模型对生成bivariate-bicycle和perturbed bivariate-bicycle码的Python程序进行变异；候选码通过分阶段验证流水线进行评估，包括GF(2)秩计算、距离估计与认证、混合整数线性规划(MILP)、BLISS Tanner图去重、可分解性分析和局部Clifford等价检查。

Result: 在码长n≤360时识别出465个不同候选码：97个CSS双变量自行车码和368个非CSS扰动变体。CSS搜索恢复了已知高性能码并发现了新的有限长表示，包括一个不可分解的[[288,16,12]]码和更高权重的d=8、k=50码。非CSS搜索产生了匹配[[144,12,12]]参数的扰动码，以及根据MILP状态报告为认证值或上界的高距离候选码。

Conclusion: LLM引导的程序进化在与独立评估配合时，可以作为一种实用的结构化量子码发现工具。

Abstract: Quantum LDPC code discovery requires searching large algebraic design spaces while reliably certifying the parameters and equivalence classes of any candidates found. We introduce an LLM-guided evolutionary workflow in which language models mutate Python programs that generate bivariate-bicycle and perturbed bivariate-bicycle code ansätze. Across five campaigns, the system performed approximately 1{,}650 evolutionary iterations, screened about $2 \times 10^5$ candidate codes, and required ${\sim}140$ hours of computation and ${\sim}$US\$400 in LLM inference cost. Candidate codes are evaluated through a staged validation pipeline combining $\mathrm{GF}(2)$ rank computation, distance estimation and certification, mixed-integer linear programming, BLISS Tanner-graph deduplication, decomposability analysis, and local-Clifford equivalence checks. At block length $n \leq 360$, the workflow identifies 465 distinct candidate codes: 97 CSS bivariate-bicycle codes and 368 non-CSS perturbed variants. The CSS search recovers known high-performing codes and finds new finite-length representatives, including an indecomposable [[288,16,12]] code and higher-weight codes with up to $k = 50$ at distance $d = 8$. The non-CSS search produces perturbed codes matching the gross-code figure of merit at [[144,12,12]], along with additional high-distance candidates reported as certified values or upper bounds according to MILP status. Overall, these results show that LLM-guided program evolution can serve as a practical tool for structured quantum-code discovery when paired with independent evaluation.

</details>


### [124] [Dynamics of the Density Cube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02421)
*Nabin Bhatta,Djordje Minic,Tatsu Takeuchi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了密度立方体理论，将量子密度矩阵扩展为三索引形式$ρ_{ijk}$，并通过三元Nambu动力学的量子化推导出其运动方程，发现三路径干涉对之间会相互振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将标准量子密度矩阵$\rho_{ij}$推广为密度立方体$\rho_{ijk}$，以描述超出传统双路径干涉的三路径干涉现象，并建立相应的动力学方程。

Method: 通过对三元Nambu动力学进行量子化，推导出密度立方体的运动方程。

Result: 密度立方体中双索引元素$\rho_{iij}$和$\rho_{ijj}$对应密度矩阵非对角元的实部和虚部，描述双路径干涉；三索引元素描述非典范的三路径干涉；且三路径干涉对之间会相互振荡。

Conclusion: 密度立方体理论扩展了量子力学的描述框架，揭示了多路径干涉的新动力学行为，为超越标准量子理论提供了可能。

Abstract: Density cube theory extends the canonical quantum density matrix $ρ_{ij}$ with the addition of an extra index to $ρ_{ijk}$. The elements of the density cube with two different indices, $ρ_{iij}$ and $ρ_{ijj}$, correspond to the real and imaginary parts of the off-diagonal element $ρ_{ij}$ of the density matrix and describe double-path interference, while those with three different indices describe non-canonical triple-path interference. In this letter, we propose an equation of motion for the density cube, obtained from the quantization of ternary Nambu dynamics, and find that pairs of triple-path interferences oscillate into each other.

</details>


### [125] [Microwave Crosstalk in Planar Superconducting Quantum Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02440)
*Yongxin Song,Dominic Hagmann,Kieran Dalton,Felix Henrich,Felix Wagner,Mohsen Bahrami Panah,Marek Pechal,Andreas Wallraff*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了超导量子器件中微波串扰的物理机制，识别出两种导致强串扰的结构（邻近驱动线和空气桥跨越耦合器），建立了定量物理模型，并提出了低串扰器件设计的指导原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微波串扰是扩展超导量子器件的主要挑战，但现有研究缺乏能够根据器件几何结构定量解释测量串扰的物理模型，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 识别了两种导致强串扰的结构（靠近量子比特的驱动线、使用空气桥跨越量子比特-量子比特耦合器的驱动线），设计并表征了包含这些结构的器件，建立了定量物理模型来解释实验观察到的串扰。

Result: 成功建立了能够定量解释实验串扰的物理模型，并基于模型提出了降低串扰的设计考量。

Conclusion: 为低串扰器件布局提供了实用指导，并为系统研究更弱串扰机制奠定了基础。

Abstract: Microwave crosstalk poses a major challenge to scaling superconducting quantum devices as it introduces excess control errors. Although its magnitude and impact have been explored in various experimental settings, quantitative physical models capable of explaining measured crosstalk for a given device geometry remain scarce. Here, we address this gap by investigating microwave crosstalk in planar superconducting devices with crossovers. We identify two structures that can lead to strong crosstalk: a drive line routed in close proximity to another qubit, and a drive line crossing a qubit-qubit coupler using an air bridge. We design and characterize devices involving these structures and develop physical models that quantitatively explain the experimentally observed crosstalk. Based on these models, we discuss the design considerations for reducing microwave crosstalk. Our results provide practical guidance for low-crosstalk device layouts and establish a basis for the systematic investigation of weaker crosstalk mechanisms.

</details>


### [126] [Closed-loop Structure of Quantum Probabilities from Unitarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02504)
*M. J. Rave*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明闭合环分解是幺正性的直接结果， Bargmann不变量自然作为与这些环相关的相位不变量出现，从而将干涉解释为不同闭合环类别的贡献，并揭示Born规则源自前向和反向振幅乘积的二次结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将闭合环视为基本量子实体并以准概率框架处理，澄清闭合环分解与量子力学基本原理（幺正性、Born规则）之间的内在联系。

Method: 从幺正性出发推导闭合环分解，建立Bargmann不变量与闭合环的自然对应关系，重新解释干涉项的物理起源。

Result: 闭合环分解是幺正性的直接推论，Bargmann不变量是环的固有相位不变量；干涉被视为不同闭合环类的贡献而非神秘交叉项；Born规则源于前向-反向振幅乘积定义的二次结构。

Conclusion: 该框架为量子概率和干涉提供了更基本的理解，统一了幺正性、相位不变性和概率幅的二次结构。

Abstract: In previous work (Rave, 2008) it was proposed that closed loops should be treated as fundamental quantum entities, and such loops were presented in a quasi-probability framework. We demonstrate that the closed-loop decomposition of quantum probabilities is a direct consequence of unitarity, and that Bargmann invariants arise naturally as the phase-invariant quantities associated with these loops, rather than being introduced independently. This identifies interference not as mysterious cross terms, but as contributions from distinct classes of closed loops weighted by their associated Bargmann phases. Additionally, the Born rule is seen to reflect the fundamental quadratic structure arising from the product of forward and reverse amplitudes, which together define such loops.

</details>


### [127] [Practical Limits on Integrated Squeezers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02524)
*Devin J. Dean,Taewon Park,Lars S. Madsen,Alex Terrasson,Sam Robison,Geun Ho Ahn,Ziyu Wang,Hubert S. Stokowski,Luke Qi,Jesse J. Slim,Joel Corney,Darwin Serkland,Warwick P. Bowen,Martin M. Fejer,Amir H. Safavi-Naeini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文量化了集成光子学中影响高度压缩真空态测量的多种噪声源的基本限制，并建立了一个通用模型，为下一代集成压缩光系统的设计和基准测试提供实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有实验成功在集成光子学中生成和检测中等程度的压缩真空态，但要利用高度压缩光降低噪声的优势，必须减轻多种噪声源的影响。作者旨在量化这些噪声源对压缩测量的基本限制，并为系统设计提供指导。

Method: 作者对不同平台和设计中的多种噪声源进行量化分析，将这些限制整合为一个简单模型，从而提供实用指导。

Result: 发现不同平台和设计中的噪声限制具有惊人的通用性，并成功将其整合为一个统一模型。

Conclusion: 该模型为下一代集成压缩光系统的设计和基准测试提供了实用指导，有助于实现更高度数的压缩光。

Abstract: Recent experiments have demonstrated the successful generation and detection of moderately squeezed vacuum states with integrated photonics. However, in order to benefit from the reduced noise of highly squeezed light, many different noise sources must be mitigated. Here, we quantify the fundamental limits these noise sources impose on squeezing measurements and find surprising generality across different platforms and designs. We combine these different limitations into a simple model that provides practical guidance for the design and benchmarking of next-generation integrated squeezed-light systems.

</details>


### [128] [Hybrid Clifford Codes via Operator Algebra Quantum Error Correction and Projective Representation Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02531)
*Jonas Eidesen,David W. Kribs,Andrew Nemec*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对Clifford码进行了双重推广，分别针对混合经典-量子信息和投影表示理论两个方向，提出了新的混合子空间和子系统Clifford码，并基于算子代数量子纠错框架扩展了基本表示论量子纠错定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Clifford码从量子稳定子码和量子子系统码的已有推广进一步扩展到混合经典-量子信息场景以及投影表示理论场景，以涵盖更广泛的编码类型。

Method: 基于表示论和算子代数量子纠错框架，对Clifford码进行双重推广：一是混合经典与量子信息编码，二是投影表示理论编码；进而构造了新的混合子空间和子系统Clifford码，并扩展了表示论量子纠错定理。

Result: 提出了混合子空间Clifford码和混合子系统Clifford码两类新码，并在算子代数框架下证明了相应的纠错定理，同时给出了若干稳定子型和非稳定子型示例。

Conclusion: 该工作统一并拓展了Clifford码的理论体系，为混合量子信息处理和投影表示理论下的纠错提供了新的构造工具和理论基础。

Abstract: Clifford codes are a natural generalization of quantum stabilizer codes based primarily on representation theory. This class of codes has previously been extended to the setting of quantum subsystem codes. We formulate a two-fold generalization of Clifford codes, for both the hybrid classical and quantum information and projective representation theory settings. This leads to new classes of hybrid subspace and subsystem Clifford codes. We extend the fundamental representation theoretic quantum error correction theorem to include these codes, based on the operator algebra quantum error correction framework. We also discuss several examples throughout the presentation, of both stabilizer and non-stabilizer type.

</details>


### [129] [Chutes and Ladders: Dynamical Automorphisms via the ZX-Calculus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02542)
*Alexander Frei,Sascha Zakaib-Bernier,Zachary Mann,Michael Vasmer,Victor V. Albert*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将ZX-演算扩展到Floquet和动态稳定子码，通过基于测量的码切换与规范固定之间的联系，实现了稳定子码空间中的闭环路径，返回原始码空间时附加一个逻辑Clifford门。这些路径可视为相对于单量子比特Clifford操作和量子比特排列的"捷径"，为构建动态自同构和搜索逻辑门实现提供了机器可解释的方法。作为示例，作者通过保距离码切换为七量子比特裸码实现了逻辑相位门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ZX-演算在量子纠错中已有诸多应用，但尚未系统性地扩展到Floquet码和动态稳定子码。同时，对于某些量子纠错码（如七量子比特裸码），基于单量子比特Clifford操作和量子比特排列无法实现非平凡逻辑Clifford门，需要更复杂的码切换机制。

Method: 利用基于测量的码切换与规范固定之间的联系（arXiv:1810.10037），将规范固定步骤组合起来形成稳定子码空间中的闭环路径；将这些路径视为相对于单量子比特Clifford操作和量子比特排列的"梯子与滑道"（shortcuts）；使用ZX-演算作为图形化语言进行形式化描述和机器可解释的计算。

Result: 提出了一个机器可解释的构建动态自同构的方法，能够系统搜索所需逻辑门的实现。作为具体实例，成功为七量子比特裸码实现了逻辑相位门，该码此前被认为没有基于单量子比特Clifford操作和量子比特排列的非平凡逻辑Clifford门。

Conclusion: ZX-演算框架可以有效地扩展到动态稳定子码领域，基于测量的码切换路径提供了一种超越单量子比特操作和排列限制的逻辑门实现方法，对量子纠错和容错量子计算具有重要价值。

Abstract: The ZX-calculus is a powerful graphical language for manipulating quantum circuits, which has recently found many applications in quantum error correction. We extend this language to handle Floquet and other dynamical stabilizer codes via the connection between measurement-based code switching and gauge fixing (arXiv:1810.10037). We combine gauge-fixing steps to implement a closed loop in the space of stabilizer codes, returning to the original codespace up to a logical Clifford gate. These measurement-based paths in the space of stabilizer codes can be viewed as shortcuts, or "chutes and ladders", relative to single-qubit Clifford operations and qubit permutations. This yields a machine-interpretable method for constructing dynamical automorphisms and facilitates the search for implementations of desired logical gates. As an example, we implement a logical phase gate via distance-preserving code switching for the seven-qubit code bare code (arXiv:1702.01155), which has no non-trivial logical Clifford gates based on single-qubit Clifford operations and qubit permutations (arXiv:2409.18175).

</details>


### [130] [Quantum Simulation of Nucleon-Antinucleon Interaction in Large-$N$ QCD$_2$ on an IBM Quantum Nighthawk Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02574)
*Cameron V. Cogburn,Sebastian Grieninger,Dmitri E. Kharzeev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在IBM量子处理器上模拟了大N二维量子色动力学中的核子-反核子相互作用，通过将问题映射为XXZ自旋链并利用变分基态和非幺正算符提取相互作用势，成功再现了吸引势行为，并与精确对角化及张量网络计算结果吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大N极限下，二维QCD可玻色子化为介子场论，重子作为拓扑孤子（扭结）出现，为研究重子-反重子动力学提供了非微扰框架。但如何在量子硬件上有效模拟这一相互作用仍是挑战。

Method: 将连续玻色子化哈密顿量映射为XXZ自旋链模型，核子/反核子对应扭结/反扭结激发；采用Jordan-Wigner变换在量子比特上实现XXZ哈密顿量；使用变分基态和经过后选择的非幺正无序算符插入来编码相互作用；利用结构化的误差抵消机制从量子硬件中鲁棒地提取相互作用势。

Result: 从量子硬件提取的扭结-反扭结相互作用势呈现预期的吸引行为；量子模拟结果与精确对角化及理想态矢量评估结果吻合良好；通过大L矩阵乘积态计算提取了连续极限下的势能。

Conclusion: 在中等规模量子处理器上实现了复杂场论中重子-反重子相互作用的可靠量子模拟，验证了基于误差抵消的量子模拟方案的有效性，为未来在量子设备上研究非微扰强相互作用开辟了路径。

Abstract: We report a quantum simulation of the nucleon--antinucleon interaction in large-$N$ two-dimensional quantum chromodynamics (QCD$_2$) on the IBM Quantum Nighthawk processor. In the large-$N$ limit, QCD$_2$ admits a bosonized description in which baryons emerge as topological solitons (kinks) of an effective mesonic field theory, providing a controlled, nonperturbative framework for baryon--antibaryon dynamics.
  We formulate the problem by mapping the continuum bosonized Hamiltonian to a spin-chain representation equivalent to an XXZ model with anisotropy set by the QCD parameters. In this mapping, nucleon and antinucleon states correspond to kink and antikink excitations, respectively, while their interaction is encoded in the spin correlations of the chain. Using Jordan--Wigner encoding, we implement the resulting XXZ Hamiltonian on a finite set of qubits and realize it via a variational ground state ansatz and postselected nonunitary disorder operator insertions optimized for the Nighthawk architecture. We then show the kink--antikink interaction potential built from the conditional energies of these nonunitary string operators can be robustly extracted from the quantum hardware due to structured error cancelation. The resulting potential exhibits the expected attractive behavior. The quantum simulation results are benchmarked against exact diagonalization, ideal statevector evaluation showing good agreement. To connect the device result to the continuum field theory, we extract the potential in the continuum limit using large-$L$ matrix product state calculations.

</details>
