### [131] [Energy spectra and cascade in the spin turbulence of a driven spinor Bose-Einstein condensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00766)
*Junghoon Lee,Jongmin Kim,Donggyu Lee,Yong-il Shin*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了驱动自旋-1玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体中的自旋湍流自旋相互作用能谱，观察到与自旋波湍流预测的-7/3幂律标度一致的结果，并发现了自旋相互作用能向高波数直接级联的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自旋张量超流体中自旋湍流的能谱特征，建立自旋湍流的能量谱标志，并实现级联动力学的定量研究。

Method: 通过对自旋-1玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体进行连续自旋驱动，产生具有空间涨落磁化的非平衡稳态，测量自旋相互作用能谱并分析其幂律标度行为；在关闭驱动后跟踪自由衰变演化以研究级联过程。

Result: 观测到与自旋波湍流预测的-7/3指数一致的幂律标度，该标度在驱动强度变化时依然保持，尽管能谱的各向异性发生了显著变化；关闭驱动后发现了自旋相互作用能向高波数的直接级联证据。

Conclusion: 实验结果建立了自旋湍流的能量谱标志，为自旋张量超流体中级联动力学的定量研究提供了基础。

Abstract: We investigate the spin-interaction energy spectrum of spin turbulence in a driven spinor Bose-Einstein condensate. Continuous spin driving of a spin-1 condensate produces a nonequilibrium steady state with spatially fluctuating magnetization. We observe a power-law scaling consistent with the $-7/3$ exponent predicted for spin-wave turbulence, which persists across our full range of drive strengths despite substantial changes in the spectral anisotropy. After switching off the drive, we track the free-decay evolution and find evidence consistent with a direct cascade of spin-interaction energy toward higher wavenumbers. These results establish an energy-spectral hallmark of spin turbulence and enable quantitative studies of cascade dynamics in spinor superfluids.

</details>


### [132] [Three- and four-boson systems expanded around the unitarity limit: Application to $^4$He](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00854)
*Feng Wu,Xincheng Lin,Ubirajara van Kolck,Sebastian König*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文在短程有效场论框架下研究了大散射长度、短有效程的三体和四体系统。在领头阶取无参数的两体幺正极限，将有限散射长度、有效程修正及重整化所需的四体力作为微扰引入次领头阶。采用Faddeev-Yakubovsky形式和图解方法，通过技术手段移除深束缚三聚体对四体计算的贡献，在更大截断下进行分析。$^4$He原子团簇的结合能和半径结果与复杂唯象势结果吻合良好，表明其物理受离散尺度不变性的小偏差支配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究大散射长度、短有效程条件下的三体和四体系统，探索离散尺度不变性及其偏离对原子团簇物理的影响，并检验短程有效场论在描述$^4$He原子团簇方面的有效性。

Method: 采用短程有效场论框架，领头阶取无参数的幺正极限，次领头阶以微扰方式引入有限散射长度、有效程修正及重整化所需的四体力。使用Faddeev-Yakubovsky形式结合图解方法，通过技术手段移除深束缚三聚体对四体计算的贡献，在更大截断下进行分析。

Result: $^4$He三体和四体原子系统的结合能和半径计算结果与使用复杂唯象势获得的结果吻合良好，且在先前FY方法无法达到的更大截断下实现了收敛。

Conclusion: $^4$He原子团簇的物理仅由离散尺度不变性的小偏差支配，短程有效场论能够有效描述这类系统。

Abstract: The three- and four-boson systems with a large scattering length and a short effective range in the two-body sector are studied in the framework of Short-Range Effective Field Theory. The starting point (leading order) of the EFT is taken to be the universal unitarity limit, where the two-body sector is parameter-free and only one three-body parameter enters. In this limit, physical systems manifests discrete scale invariance. Deviations from universality arising from finite scattering-length and effective-range corrections, as well as a four-body force required by renormalization, are included perturbatively at next-to-leading order. The three-body ground state and its associated four-body ground and first-excited states are studied using the Faddeev-Yakubovsky formalism and a complementary diagrammatic approach. By employing techniques to remove contributions from deep trimers in tetramer calculations, we extend our analysis to larger cutoffs than previously accessible within the FY approach. Our results for binding energies and radii of $^4$He three- and four-atom systems converge well to results obtained with sophisticated phenomenological potentials. These successes suggest that the physics of $^4$He atomic clusters is governed by only small deviations from discrete scale invariance.

</details>


### [133] [Ground-state phase diagram of Rydberg atoms in a triangular-prism array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01116)
*Qing-Yuan Zuo,Shuo Geng,Shan-Wen Tsai,Jin Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 利用密度矩阵重正化群，研究了三角棱柱光镊阵列中里德伯原子的基态相图。通过调节失谐-拉比频率比和里德伯封锁半径，系统实现了多种自发破缺平移和腿交换对称性的密度波相。三角棱柱具有D₃对称性，比两腿梯子更丰富，观察到多种有序相及相变，包括Z₂×Z₃破缺相、D₃破缺相、不同周期的梯级三聚化密度波等，涉及BKT、伊辛、手征临界线及Potts和Ashkin-Teller点等丰富临界行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索三角棱柱几何（D₃对称性）中里德伯原子阵列的基态相图，研究比简单两腿梯子（Z₂对称性）更丰富的对称性破缺和量子相变行为。

Method: 使用密度矩阵重正化群（DMRG）进行数值模拟，通过调节失谐与拉比频率之比以及里德伯封锁半径来遍历相空间。

Result: 发现了多种密度波相：(1) 中等封锁半径下，大失谐时出现交替双占据和单占据相，破缺Z₂平移和Z₃旋转对称性；(2) 该相通过两次BKT转变进入中间临界相，由Z₆时钟模型描述；(3) 大封锁半径下，出现每个三角形一个里德伯激发的D₃破缺相（一级相变）；(4) 抑制相邻三角形双占据时，出现梯级三聚化密度波，其熔化行为对应Z₂（伊辛临界线）、Z₃/Z₄（手征临界线，含Potts和Ashkin-Teller点）；(5) Z₂梯级三聚化相内部存在纠缠熵峰，标志增强周期-2密度调制的交叉区，之后一级相变进入Z₂×D₃相；(6) 不同周期三聚化态之间出现非公度准长程序的浮动相。

Conclusion: 三角棱柱阵列因其D₃对称性展现出比两腿梯子更丰富的相图和临界行为，为研究超越Z₂对称性的量子多体物理提供了新的平台。

Abstract: We study the ground-state phase diagram of Rydberg atoms in a triangular-prism optical tweezer array using the density matrix renormalization group. By tuning the detuning-to-Rabi-frequency ratio and the Rydberg blockade radius, the system realizes several density-wave phases with spontaneous breaking of translational and leg-exchange symmetries. Unlike two-leg Rydberg ladders with $\mathbb{Z}_2$ leg-exchange symmetry, the triangular prism has $\mathbb{D}_3$ symmetry, leading to a richer set of ordered phases and transitions. For blockade radius moderately larger than the lattice spacing, a phase with alternating double and single Rydberg occupancy appears at large detuning. It breaks $\mathbb{Z}_2$ translational and $\mathbb{Z}_3$ rotational symmetry while preserving a rung reflection symmetry. Upon decreasing detuning, it melts through two Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless transitions with an intermediate critical phase described by a $\mathbb{Z}_6$ clock model. At larger blockade radius, a phase with one Rydberg excitation per triangle and broken $\mathbb{D}_3$ symmetry appears through a first-order transition. When double occupation of neighboring triangles is suppressed, rung-trimerized density waves develop as detuning increases from the disordered phase. Their melting follows the same structure as in Rydberg chains and two-leg ladders: the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ case has Ising critical lines, while the $\mathbb{Z}_3$ and $\mathbb{Z}_4$ cases have chiral critical lines, with Potts and Ashkin-Teller points only on the corresponding commensurate lines. Inside the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ rung-trimerized phase, an entanglement-entropy peak signals a crossover regime with enhanced period-2 density modulation before a first-order transition into a $\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{D}_3$ phase. Floating phases with incommensurate quasi-long-range order appear between trimerized states of different periods.

</details>


### [134] [Negative Interaction Quench Dynamics of Density-Ordered Dipolar Bosons in a One-Dimensional Optical Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01712)
*Rhombik Roy,N. D. Chavda,Barnali Chakrabarti,Arnaldo Gammal*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文利用多组态含时Hartree方法（MCTDHB）数值研究了有限一维光晶格中密度有序的偶极玻色气体在负相互作用淬火后的非平衡动力学。发现尽管系统表现出强烈的动力学响应，但晶体态关联对淬火具有鲁棒性，并识别出局域呼吸模式和偶极振荡等涌现激发模式，同时揭示了隧穿与关联动力学之间的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究密度有序的偶极玻色气体在相互作用符号反转（从长程到短程相互作用）的淬火后的非平衡动力学行为，理解晶格系统中关联与隧穿动力学的相互作用。

Method: 采用多组态含时Hartree方法（MCTDHB）对有限一维光晶格中的偶极玻色气体进行精确数值模拟，通过负相互作用淬火驱动系统从长程跨越到短程相互作用，结合实空间和动量空间可观测量以及Glauber一阶和二阶关联函数分析动力学行为。

Result: 发现了丰富的阱内和阱间隧穿动力学，涵盖超流、Mott绝缘体和碎裂态等多种量子态；晶体态关联对淬火表现出鲁棒性；识别出局域呼吸模式和偶极振荡等激发模式；通过位点分辨的位置方差量化了隧穿过程；证实了隧穿与关联动力学之间的直接联系。

Conclusion: 偶极晶格系统可通过相互作用淬火与晶格深度斜坡相结合实现可控的动力学工程，为非平衡量子模拟提供了有前景的平台。

Abstract: We explore the nonequilibrium dynamics of a density-ordered dipolar Bose gas in a finite one-dimensional optical lattice following a negative interaction quench, using the numerically exact multiconfigurational time-dependent Hartree method for bosons. The interaction sign reversal, effectively driving a crossover from long-range to short-range interactions, generates rich intra- and interwell tunneling dynamics spanning superfluid, Mott-insulating, and fragmented regimes. A striking finding is the robustness of the underlying crystal-state correlations against the quench, despite the strong dynamical response. We identify emergent excitation modes, including local breathing and dipole-like oscillations, via real- and momentum-space observables, and quantify tunneling through site-resolved position variance. One- and two-body Glauber correlation functions further uncover a direct connection between tunneling and correlation dynamics. Moreover, we show that combining interaction quenches with lattice-depth ramping enables controllable dynamical engineering, establishing dipolar lattice systems as a promising platform for nonequilibrium quantum simulation.

</details>


### [135] [Microscopic Origin of Emergent Elliptic Flow and Molecule Formation in Strongly Interacting Quasi-Two-Dimensional Few-Body Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01728)
*Xin-Yuan Gao,Kai Yuen Lee,Qingze Guan,Yangqian Yan*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文使用含时显式关联高斯(TDECG)方法定量重现了二维少体费米子系统的椭圆流实验观测结果，并通过多体波函数和两点关联函数揭示了从强相互作用气体到配对分子流的动力学BCS-BEC渡越过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期少体费米子实验观测到了相互作用驱动的椭圆流现象，但由于维数灾难和强关联效应，捕捉两粒子以上系统的这种流体动力学行为在理论上极具挑战性。

Method: 采用从头算含时显式关联高斯(TDECG)方法，使用中等数量的关联高斯基函数，对最多六粒子系统进行动力学可观测量计算。

Result: 成功定量重现实验观测结果；获得了最多六粒子系统的收敛动力学可观测量；通过多体波函数和两点关联函数实时可视化展示了动力学BCS-BEC渡越过程。

Conclusion: TDECG方法能够有效克服维数灾难和强关联效应带来的挑战，为少体费米子系统中的流体动力学现象提供定量理论描述，并揭示其背后的微观机制。

Abstract: Recent experiments simulating two-dimensional few-fermion systems have observed emergent hydrodynamic behavior, i.e., interaction-driven elliptic flow by adding fermions two at a time [S.~Brandstetter et al., Nat. Phys. (2025)]. Due to the curse of dimensionality and strong correlations, capturing such phenomena beyond two particles remains challenging. Here, we use the ab initio time-dependent explicitly correlated Gaussian (TDECG) method to quantitatively reproduce these experimental observations. With only a moderate number of correlated Gaussian basis functions, our approach obtains converged dynamical observables for systems up to six particles. Furthermore, real-time access to the many-body wavefunction and two-point correlation functions enables us to visualize the transformation from a strongly interacting gas to a stream of paired molecules, i.e., a dynamical BCS-BEC crossover.

</details>


### [136] [Noise spectroscopy of two-body loss as a probe of dynamical bulk viscosity in ultracold atomic gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01835)
*Tingyu Zhang,Hiroyuki Tajima,Takeo Kato*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文证明了弱耗散量子气体中两体损耗电流的关联噪声可用于探测动态体粘滞性。通过从Lindblad方程出发推导损耗电流算符，并扣除泊松散粒噪声背景后，发现剩余噪声功率谱正比于接触算符的平衡关联函数。结合接触关联与体粘滞性的精确关系，建立了可测量的损耗电流噪声与动态体粘滞性之间的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态体粘滞性在实验中一直难以测量，作者旨在寻找一种新的可观测探针来解决这一长期存在的实验难题。

Method: 从弱耗散量子气体的Lindblad方程出发，推导了两体损耗电流算符；通过扣除泊松散粒噪声背景，分析了剩余噪声功率谱与接触算符平衡关联函数的关系；并结合接触关联与体粘滞性的精确关系。

Result: 两体损耗电流的剩余噪声功率谱正比于接触算符的平衡关联函数，进而与动态体粘滞性建立对应关系。

Conclusion: 两体损耗的高阶涨落可作为动态体粘滞性的实验探针，解决了该量长期难以测量的难题。

Abstract: We show that the correlated noise of the two-body loss current provides access to the dynamical bulk viscosity in weakly dissipative quantum gases. Starting from the Lindblad equation for weak inelastic losses, we derive the loss-current operator. After subtracting the leading Poissonian shot-noise background, the remaining noise power spectrum of two-body loss current is found proportional to the equilibrium correlation function of the contact operator. Combining this result with the exact relation between contact correlations and bulk viscosity, we demonstrate the correspondence between the measurable loss-current noise and the bulk-viscosity. Our result identifies the higher-order fluctuation of two-body loss as a probe of dynamical bulk viscosity, whose measurement has remained elusive in experiments.

</details>


### [137] [Vibrational resonance in a one-dimensional dissipative Bose-Josephson junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02157)
*Abhik Kumar Saha*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了耗散玻色-约瑟夫森结在弱低频探测和高频驱动共同作用下的线性和非线性响应，发现高频调制可动态破缺对称性并诱导二次谐波响应，且响应幅度在最优驱动强度下出现共振增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一维耗散玻色-约瑟夫森结在同时受弱低频探测和快速振荡高频驱动时，其线性和非线性响应特性，并揭示高频场对系统对称性及共振行为的调控机制。

Method: 从耗散双模玻色-约瑟夫森方程出发，保留主导非线性修正项推导出有效高阶非线性方程；利用时间尺度分离和微扰分析，分别获得基频线性响应和二次谐波非线性响应的解析表达式。

Result: 高频调制改变了有效势能景观并动态破缺了定态周围的对称性，产生无高频场时不存在有限二次谐波响应；线性和非线性响应幅度均在最优高频驱动强度下表现出类似共振的增强；分析了共振特性对相互作用强度、耗散和驱动参数的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 研究结果展示了一种在耗散玻色-约瑟夫森结中实现线性和非线性振动共振的可控机制，为操控驱动超冷玻色系统中的集体动力学开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We investigate the linear and nonlinear response of a one-dimensional dissipative Bose-Josephson junction subjected simultaneously to a weak low-frequency probe and a rapidly oscillating high-frequency external drive. Starting from the dissipative two-mode Bose-Josephson equations, we derive an effective higher-order nonlinear equation for the population imbalance by retaining the leading nonlinear correction. Using time-scale separation and perturbative analysis, we obtain analytical expressions for both the linear response at the fundamental frequency and the nonlinear response at the second harmonic. We show that the high-frequency modulation modifies the effective potential landscape and dynamically breaks the symmetry around the stationary state, giving rise to a finite second-harmonic response that is absent without the rapidly oscillating field. Both the linear and nonlinear response amplitudes exhibit resonance-like enhancement for optimal values of the high-frequency driving strength. We further analyze the dependence of the resonance characteristics on interaction strength, dissipation, and driving parameters in both the zero-phase and $π$-phase modes and compare the analytical predictions with direct numerical simulations. Our results demonstrate a controllable mechanism toward realizing linear and nonlinear vibrational resonance in a one-dimensional dissipative Bose-Josephson junction and open new possibilities for controlling collective dynamics in driven ultracold bosonic systems.

</details>


### [138] [Chaotic spin dynamics of elongated spinor condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02456)
*Jose Reyes-Calderón,Albert Gallemí,Carsten Klempt,Luis Santos*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了细长自旋-1玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体中局部磁化动力学，揭示了不同动力学区域的共存、由空间激发态量子相变形成的鲁棒界面，以及混沌动力学行为，并绘制了可实验探测的普适相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于非均匀密度分布导致的非线性与量子效应相互作用，细长自旋-1凝聚体呈现出高度非平凡的局部磁化动力学，理解这些动力学现象是本文的核心动机。

Method: 通过对初始全局淬火后系统的动力学分析，识别出不同动力学区域，利用空间激发态量子相变描述区域间的鲁棒界面，并通过映射普适相图区分规则与混沌动力学区域。

Result: 发现了不同动力学区域在同一系统中的共存现象；鲁棒界面充当空间激发态量子相变；局部自旋动力学可进入混沌状态，表现为不规则演化和对初始条件的指数敏感性；得到了区分规则与混沌区域的普适相图。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示的普适相图可在正在进行的实验中进行探测，为理解自旋-1凝聚体中的复杂动力学提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Elongated spin-$1$ condensates present a highly non-trivial local magnetization dynamics, due to the interplay between nonlinear and quantum effects stemming from the inhomogeneous density profile. This interplay results in different dynamical regimes after an initial global quench. In particular, we show that the system may display the coexistence of markedly different dynamical domains separated by a robust interface that acts as a spatial excited-state quantum phase transition. Furthermore, the local spinor dynamics may enter a chaotic regime characterized by irregular evolution and exponential sensitivity to initial conditions. We map the universal phase diagram distinguishing regular and chaotic regimes, which may be probed in on-going experiments.

</details>


### [139] [Thouless Pumping of Large Chern Numbers in Optical Floquet Quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02489)
*Shien Wan,Zecheng Li,Bo Song*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文提出在光学Floquet准晶中实现大陈数的方案，并通过Thouless泵浦直接测量，揭示了输运特征与准能谱的联系，为探索丰富拓扑动力学和实现分数量子陈绝缘态开辟了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数拓扑系统的陈数仅为单位量级，现有方案难以实现大陈数，本文旨在光学Floquet准晶中实现大陈数并可直接测量。

Method: 利用Floquet驱动在准晶中诱导拓扑能带，通过能隙标记定理表征陈数，并研究不同驱动频率和幅度下的Thouless泵浦行为。

Result: 在Floquet准晶中实现了大陈数，并建立了输运特征与准能谱之间的对应关系。

Conclusion: 该方案为探索Floquet准晶中丰富的拓扑动力学以及实现分数量子陈绝缘态提供了新途径。

Abstract: Chern numbers are central to correlated and topological phenomena, yet most topological systems are associated with Chern numbers of order unity. Here we propose a scheme to achieve large Chern numbers in an optical Floquet quasicrystal with cold atoms, which can be directly measured via Thouless pumping. We study the quasienergy spectrum of Floquet quasicrystals and characterize the emergent Chern numbers using gap labeling theorem. We further investigate the Thouless pumping in the Floquet quasicrystal at different driving frequencies and amplitudes, revealing the connection between transport features and the quasienergy spectrum. Our findings open new avenues for exploring rich topological dynamics in Floquet quasicrystals and realizing fractional Chern insulating states.

</details>


### [140] [Aharonov-Casher Chern bands for ultracold dark state atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00207)
*Domantas Burba,Dominykas Borodinas,Gediminas Juzeliūnas*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了超冷原子在Λ型原子-光耦合方案中绝热跟随暗态时的Aharonov-Casher条件，推导了该条件成立的通用要求，并分析了精细调谐与有限耦合强度两种不完美因素如何组合以实现完全平坦的最低能带，为模拟分数量子霍尔态提供了拓扑完美的平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在Λ型原子-光耦合方案中，超冷原子绝热跟随暗态时Aharonov-Casher条件的一般性成立要求，并研究如何通过调控不完美因素实现可用于模拟分数量子霍尔态的完全平坦最低能带。

Method: 理论推导了AC条件对原子-光耦合的通用要求（拉比频率为平面波的叠加），分析了N=3,4,6个精细调谐平面波产生的平滑背景磁场及Aharonov-Bohm通量奇点，并研究了偏离精细调谐和有限耦合强度两种不完美因素对最低能带结构的影响。

Result: 精细调谐时产生具有确定符号的平滑背景磁场和不可测量的AB通量奇点；偏离精细调谐使奇点拓宽为亚波长尺度的反向磁场斑块，展宽最低能带；精细调谐下有限耦合强度也会展宽能带；两种不完美因素的适当组合可产生完全平坦且具有完美拓扑结构的最低能带。

Conclusion: 通过适当组合偏离精细调谐和有限原子-光耦合强度这两种不完美因素，可以实现完全平坦且拓扑完美的最低能带，为模拟分数量子霍尔效应提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We consider the Aharonov-Casher (AC) condition for ultracold atoms adiabatically following the dark-state in a $Λ$-type atom-light coupling scheme. The AC condition establishes a relation between the geometric scalar potential and the synthetic magnetic field, resulting in a fully degenerate lowest Landau-level-like band even if the magnetic field is inhomogeneous but has a proper sign. We derive a general requirement for the atom-light coupling under which the AC condition applies. The requirement holds if the Rabi frequencies of the $Λ$ scheme are the superposition of plane waves with the appropriate amplitudes and phases. In particular, the Rabi frequencies made of $N=3,\,4,\,6$ fine tuned plane waves yield a smooth background magnetic field of definite sign, as well as an array of non-measurable Aharonov-Bohm flux singularities. Departing from the fine tuning, the latter singularities broaden into narrow subwavelength patches of the opposite magnetic field, which broaden the lowest energy band. The lowest band is broadened also for the fine tuned situation due to deviation from the adiabatic approach because of the finite atom-light coupling strength. It is shown that a proper combination of the two imperfections (departure from fine tuning and finite atom-light coupling strength) can lead to a completely flat lowest band, which furthermore is characterized by the perfect topology needed for simulating the fractional Hall states.

</details>

### [141] [The Longest Increasing Subsequence Problem revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01501)
*Silvio Franz,Roberto Mulet*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究最长递增子序列问题的热力学景观，发现了两种能量尺度（Schottky交叉和凝聚相变），并证明在低温下局部蒙特卡洛动力学陷入具有玻璃态特征的亚稳态，揭示了热力学稀疏性而非能量壁垒导致局部搜索的动态难解性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究最长递增子序列这一经典组合优化问题的热力学景观，理解其与统计力学中玻璃态现象之间的深层联系，揭示为何存在多项式时间精确算法的问题在局部搜索动力学下仍会表现出难解性。

Method: 引入温度参数构建热力学框架，识别两种不同的能量尺度（T_cross处的Schottky交叉和T_cond处的凝聚相变）；通过局部蒙特卡洛动力学模拟低温淬火过程，观测亚稳态行为；采用对数退火策略对比平衡态结果。

Result: 发现T_cond以下最大长度构型数量呈亚指数增长；低温淬火后局部动力学表现出玻璃态特征：两步弛豫、持续动力学重叠和老化现象；对数退火能追踪平衡态直至基态。

Conclusion: 热力学稀疏性（而非能量壁垒）是导致局部搜索动态难解的根本原因，LIS问题可作为精确可解优化与玻璃态自旋玻璃现象之间的桥梁。

Abstract: The Longest Increasing Subsequence problem - a classic combinatorial challenge with deep connections to statistical mechanics- exhibits a rich thermodynamic landscape. Introducing a temperature we identify two distinct energy scales: A Schottky-like crossover at T_cross and a condensation transition at T_cond, below which the number of maximum-length configurations becomes sub-exponential in system size. We also show that despite the existence of polynomial-time dynamic programming algorithms for the ground state, local Monte Carlo dynamics, after sudden quenches at low temperatures, become trapped in metastable state configurations displaying characteristic glassy signatures: two-step relaxation, persistent dynamical overlaps and aging. On the other hand, logarithmic annealing tracks equilibrium down to the ground state. These results establish that thermodynamic sparsity - not energetic barriers - can render local search dynamically intractable, positioning the LIS problem as a bridge between exactly solvable optimization and glassy spin-glass phenomenology.

</details>


### [142] [A Minimal Duality Estimate for the Surface-Code Threshold under Nearest-Neighbor Correlated Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02052)
*Masayuki Ohzeki*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文利用单方程对偶准则，对从具有最近邻相关误差的表面码精确误差边映射得到的方八角随机键伊辛模型进行了分析，计算了误差边约化后的最小胞元，在对称情况下得到临界概率p_c=0.0288427147，与约3%的数值阈值高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证单方程对偶准则在方八角随机键伊辛模型中的适用性，并与表面码的数值阈值进行对比，以确认该方法的有效性。

Method: 采用单方程对偶准则，对从表面码精确误差边映射导出的方八角随机键伊辛模型进行分析，计算误差边约化后最小胞元的临界概率。

Result: 在对称情况(p1=p2=p3=p)下，得到临界概率p_c=0.0288427147，与先前报道的约3%的数值阈值高度吻合。

Conclusion: 单方程对偶准则能够精确预测表面码在最近邻相关误差下的阈值，验证了该理论方法的可靠性。

Abstract: We apply the single-equation duality criterion to the square-octagonal random-bond Ising model recently obtained from an exact error-edge map for a surface code with nearest-neighbor correlated errors. The calculation is performed for the minimal cell after the error-edge reduction. For the symmetric case \(p_1=p_2=p_3=p\), this gives \(p_c=0.0288427147\), in close agreement with the reported numerical threshold of about \(3\%\).

</details>


### [143] [Probing the scale-free hierarchy of the $p=3$ spherical spin glass via persistent Langevin dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02239)
*Li Zhenpeng*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究持久朗之万动力学下的p=3球面自旋玻璃模型，发现能量关联时间tau_corr与持久时间tau_p之间满足tau_corr ~ tau_p^α的标度关系，指数α=1/3，与KPZ普适类预测一致；温度依赖性呈现U形曲线，揭示了弹道/惯性、KPZ和噪声主导三种动力学区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究持久随机游走者如何感知复杂能量景观，特别是通过调节持久时间tau_p控制能量分辨率来揭示隐藏的景观拓扑结构。

Method: 采用持久朗之万动力学模拟p=3球面自旋玻璃模型，通过改变持久时间tau_p（控制能量分辨率δE∝1/tau_p），测量能量关联时间tau_corr，并进行有限尺寸标度分析。

Result: 在T=1.0时，tau_corr~tau_p^α，α=0.337±0.035（N=64, tau_p∈[2,32]），与KPZ普适类预测的1/3高度一致；有限尺寸标度给出热力学极限α(∞)=0.3333±0.0134；温度依赖α(T)呈现U形曲线。

Conclusion: 持久朗之万动力学是揭示隐藏景观拓扑结构的有效工具；p=3球面自旋玻璃属于KPZ普适类。

Abstract: How does a persistent random walker perceive a complex energy landscape? We address this question by studying the persistent Langevin dynamics of the p=3 spherical spin glass, a paradigmatic mean-field model with a scale-free hierarchical landscape. By tuning the persistence time tau_p, which controls the walker's inertia and effectively sets its energy resolution delta E proportional to 1/tau_p, we measure the energy correlation time tau_corr. At temperature T=1.0, we find tau_corr scales as tau_p to the power alpha with alpha = 0.337 plus or minus 0.035 for tau_p in the range [2,32] (for N=64), in excellent agreement with the Kardar-Parisi-Zhang (KPZ) universality class prediction alpha = 1/3. Finite-size scaling using N = 16,32,48,64,128 yields the thermodynamic limit alpha(infinity) = 0.3333 plus or minus 0.0134, fully consistent with 1/3. Thus, tau_p acts as a tunable probe that reveals the predicted scale-free hierarchy of the landscape. Moreover, the temperature dependence alpha(T) for T = 0.5,1.0,1.5,2.0 exhibits a clear U-shaped curve, identifying three dynamical regimes: ballistic/inertial, KPZ, and noise-dominated. Our results establish persistent Langevin dynamics as a powerful tool for uncovering hidden landscape topology and demonstrate that the p=3 spherical spin glass belongs to the KPZ universality class.

</details>


### [144] [Frustrated neurons: Energy landscapes and relaxation dynamics in repulsive phase oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02512)
*Brandon B. Le*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文将几何阻挫这一凝聚态物理核心范式引入神经时序研究，构建了受挫神经节律的最小理论，将排斥耦合的节律单元映射为反铁磁XY模型。通过分析三角形、四面体和kagome晶格等几何结构，揭示了零温弛豫倾向于选择低能亚稳态而非精确基态，并指出神经系统中弱全局相干性可能反映受挫动力学景观下结构化的局部时序有序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为神经系统中普遍存在的弱全局相干性和丰富局部时序结构提供统一的理论解释框架，将凝聚态物理中成熟的几何阻挫概念转化为神经相位动力学的诊断工具。

Method: 构建排斥耦合振子到反铁磁XY模型的映射，利用凝聚态物理中的局域约束、简并基态流形、亚稳态和淬火动力学等概念，对不同几何结构（三角形、四面体、kagome晶格）上的神经相位动力学进行理论分析。

Result: 识别了三角形中两个手性120°时序态作为最小阻挫基元；发现四面体的约化基态流形由与对极点配对相关的连续分支相交构成；kagome晶格上局域约束定义了三色流形，零温弛豫抑制全局同步但选择低能亚稳态（力矩平衡态）而非精确基态。

Conclusion: 神经系统中弱全局相干性并非活动无序的信号，而是受挫动力学景观下结构化局部时序有序的体现。该相位理论可作为有效相互作用目标，将几何时序阻挫通过闭回路上偏好相位延迟的不相容性映射回生物物理神经模型。

Abstract: Geometrical frustration, a central paradigm in condensed matter physics, provides a unifying language for systems in which locally preferred interactions cannot be made globally compatible. Here, we use this language to formulate a minimal theory of frustrated neural timing, mapping repulsively coupled rhythmic units onto antiferromagnetic XY models. Within this framework, the condensed-matter concepts of local constraints, degenerate ground-state manifolds, metastability, and quench dynamics become a concrete diagnostic framework for structured neural phase dynamics. We analyze a hierarchy of geometries: a triangle as the minimal frustrated motif with two chiral 120° timing states, a tetrahedron whose reduced ground-state manifold consists of intersecting continuous branches associated with antipodal pairings, and a kagome lattice on which local constraints define a constrained three-coloring manifold. The kagome lattice reveals the central dynamical result: zero-temperature relaxation suppresses global synchrony but typically selects low-energy metastable torque-balanced states rather than exact ground states. Finally, we show how the phase theory can be carried back towards biophysical neural models by treating it as an effective-interaction target, where geometrical timing frustration is realized through preferred phase lags that become incompatible around closed motifs. This perspective suggests that weak global coherence in neural systems does not necessarily signal disordered activity, but can reflect structured local timing order shaped by a frustrated dynamical landscape.

</details>


### [145] [Localization of Active Particles on Random Arrays of Parallel Filaments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00286)
*Owen Santoso,Elena Koslover*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 研究无序微管阵列中间歇运动粒子的局域化现象，发现粒子会在丝状物汇聚区域聚集，并在中等运行长度时局域化最强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受哺乳动物树突中平行微管混合极性排列的启发，探索无序丝状物阵列如何改变间歇运动粒子的输运动力学和空间分布。

Method: 在快速附着-解离极限下，将无序系统映射为含噪声的一维有效能量景观，其结构近似为随机游走，并建立势阱深度和宽度与输运动力学和系统几何参数的关系。

Result: 无序阵列导致粒子在丝状物汇聚区域局域化；中等运行长度时局域化最强，此时偏向性输运能感知淬火极性但不足以逃逸局部陷阱。

Conclusion: 随机丝状物网络上粒子的鲁棒局域化源于主动输运与淬火无序的相互作用，揭示了涌现的空间组织机制。

Abstract: Quenched disorder in the environment can fundamentally alter transport dynamics in both active and passive systems. We explore how disordered arrays of filaments govern the distribution of intermittently moving particles which switch between diffusive and processive transport. Motivated by the mixed-polarity arrangements of parallel microtubules observed in mammalian dendrites, we show that such arrays tend to result in localization of particles at regions of convergent filament orientation. In the rapid attachment-detachment limit, the disordered system can be described by a noisy one-dimensional effective energy landscape, whose structure is approximated by a random walk. The depth and width of wells on this landscape are expressed as a function of the transport kinetics and system geometry. Localization is shown to be strongest at intermediate run-lengths, where biased transport persists long enough to sense the quenched filament polarity but not so long as to facilitate escape from local traps. These results demonstrate robust localization of particles moving on random filament networks, highlighting the emergent spatial organization that arises from an interplay of active transport and quenched disorder.

</details>

### [146] [Non-equilibrium glassy arrest and discontinuous transitions at avoided quantum critical points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00580)
*J J Pulikkotil*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了在外部约束场作用下，序参量在空间扩展的Belitz-Kirkpatrick-Vojta框架中的非平衡动力学。在避免临界性处，由于宏观自由能平坦化与微观无序诱导钉扎的相互作用，出现了通用的非单调耗散峰。靠近该区域时，被抑制的确定力增强了激活钉扎，导致涨落-耗散定理有效破坏并最终形成玻璃态阻滞。在更高场下，系统经历不连续相变，绕过了平坦自由能区域。这些显式解析标度建立了无序凝聚态系统中非平衡阻滞的通用机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解无序凝聚态系统中非平衡阻滞（如玻璃态行为）的通用物理机制，特别是外部约束场如何通过自由能地形平坦化与微观无序钉扎的协同作用导致动力学停滞。

Method: 在空间扩展的Belitz-Kirkpatrick-Vojta理论框架中，解析研究序参量在外部约束场下的非平衡动力学，分析自由能地形、耗散峰及相变行为。

Result: 发现了在避免临界性处出现非单调耗散峰的通用现象；阐明了自由能平坦化与无序诱导钉扎导致涨落-耗散定理破坏及玻璃态阻滞的机制；揭示了高场下不连续相变可绕过平坦自由能区域。

Conclusion: 提出了无序凝聚态系统中非平衡阻滞的通用解析标度机制，为理解玻璃态动力学和类似非平衡停滞现象提供了理论框架。

Abstract: The non-equilibrium dynamics of an order parameter confined by an external constraint field are investigated within a spatially extended Belitz-Kirkpatrick-Vojta framework. A generic non-monotonic dissipation peak arises at avoided criticality due to the interplay between macroscopic free-energy flattening and microscopic disorder-induced trapping. Near this regime, suppressed deterministic forces enhance activated trapping, leading to an effective violation of the fluctuation-dissipation theorem and ultimate glassy arrest. At higher fields, the system undergoes a discontinuous phase transition that bypasses this flat free-energy region. These explicit analytical scalings establish a generic mechanism for non-equilibrium arrest in disordered condensed matter systems.

</details>


### [147] [Wasserstein-2 gradient flows and the geometry of entropy production in classical and quantum stochastic thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00698)
*Olga Movilla Miangolarra,Ralph Sabbagh,Artemy Kolchinsky*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文基于Wasserstein-2度量和自由能梯度流，为经典和量子热力学中的熵产生建立了统一的几何描述，将纯耗散动力学推广至包含保守（哈密顿）动力学的体系，并建立了与线性响应热力学长度和最优输运理论的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热力学第二定律不仅指明了演化方向，还赋予了非平衡变换潜在的几何结构。现有理论在纯耗散系统（过阻尼扩散、细致平衡马尔可夫链、耗散林德布拉德动力学）中已有梯度流描述，但缺乏一个统一框架来涵盖同时包含耗散和保守（哈密顿）动力学的系统（如经典惯性系统和开放量子系统），并建立几何结构与熵产生、热力学长度、最优协议之间的联系。

Method: 1. 回顾过阻尼扩散、细致平衡马尔可夫链和耗散林德布拉德动力学中自由能梯度流与Wasserstein-2度量的关系；2. 引入广义Wasserstein-2度量，将保守（哈密顿）动力学纳入几何框架，分别处理经典惯性系统和开放量子系统；3. 推导纯耗散几何与哈密顿-耗散混合几何之间的等价性界限，量化惯性或相干动力学对耗散的减少；4. 在平衡分布限制下恢复线性响应热力学长度（包括量子热力学长度），建立与最优输运和逆绝热协议的关联。

Result: 建立了统一几何框架，其中Wasserstein-2距离可约束熵产生，给出第二定律的有限时间精化；广义度量可精确刻画固定耗散迁移率下的最小熵产生；证明了惯性/相干动力学可减少耗散；恢复了经典和量子热力学长度，将最优输运、热力学长度和逆绝热协议统一在同一几何框架中。

Conclusion: 本文扩展了热力学的黎曼计划，使其进一步远离平衡态，为超越过阻尼设定的最优协议提供了几何基础，实现了对经典和量子非平衡热力学中熵产生几何的完整统一描述。

Abstract: The second law does more than set the direction of thermodynamic evolution: it endows nonequilibrium transformations with an underlying geometry. In this work, we provide a unified geometric description of entropy production in classical and quantum thermodynamics based on Wasserstein-2 structures arising from gradient flows of free energy. We review how relaxation to equilibrium, in overdamped diffusions, discrete detailed-balanced Markov chains, and dissipative Lindblad dynamics, can be formulated as a gradient flow on the space of states. The associated Wasserstein-2 distance bounds entropy production, yielding a finite-time refinement of the second law. We extend this framework beyond purely dissipative dynamics by introducing generalized Wasserstein-2 metrics that incorporate conservative (Hamiltonian) dynamics in both classical inertial systems and open quantum systems, yielding intrinsic distances that exactly characterize minimal entropy production under fixed dissipative mobilities. We establish equivalence bounds between purely dissipative and Hamiltonian-dissipative geometries, explicitly quantifying how inertial or coherent dynamics can reduce dissipation. Finally, when restricted to equilibrium distributions, we recover the thermodynamic length of linear response-including the quantum thermodynamic length-thereby linking optimal transport, thermodynamic length, and counterdiabatic protocols within a single geometric framework. All in all, our results extend the Riemannian program of thermodynamics further from equilibrium and provide a geometric foundation for optimal protocols beyond the overdamped setting.

</details>


### [148] [Pre-failure response spectra predict finite-amplitude fragility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00789)
*Surachate Limkumnerd*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出“脆弱性曲线”概念，通过线性响应谱宽度预测非线性系统的有限振幅脆弱性，证明响应通道越宽（即使最强方向增益固定），系统越容易在小扰动下失效。在非线性非正态网络和交通流瓶颈中验证了该机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统失效理论仅关注单一最危险路径（最不稳定模态、最弱链接、最小作用量逃逸路径或最优扰动），但有限振幅敏感性不仅取决于最近路径，还取决于扰动空间中靠近危险方向的范围有多大。本文旨在将这种区别形式化为脆弱性问题。

Method: 提出脆弱性曲线：对于每个扰动方向，失效距离是越过指定边界的最小振幅；脆弱性曲线是低于给定振幅的失效方向比例。证明该曲线可由失效前的单一量——边界归一化脆弱性增益（基于线性化响应）的尾部预测。响应谱的宽度决定最强方向之外有多少近危险路径共存。

Result: 在高维非线性非正态网络中，固定最强方向增益的情况下，响应通道更宽的系统具有更大的非线性脆弱性曲线，将宽度与最差方向解耦。在确定性交通流崩溃的独立标量测试中，匹配最强响应后，响应宽度降低校准的拥堵阈值，残余边际筛选但从不逆转该效应。

Conclusion: 响应谱宽度成为超越最强路径的有限振幅脆弱性的失效前坐标。

Abstract: Failure theories often identify a single leading route to failure: the most unstable mode, weakest link, minimum-action escape path, or optimal perturbation. Yet finite-amplitude susceptibility depends not only on the nearest route but on how much of perturbation space lies near dangerous directions. We cast this distinction as a fragility problem: for each perturbation direction, the failure distance is the smallest amplitude that crosses a prescribed boundary, and the fragility curve is the fraction of directions that fail below a given amplitude. Measuring this curve directly requires nonlinear trials over many directions; instead, we show that it is predicted, before any failure occurs, by the tail of a single pre-failure quantity: the boundary-normalized fragility gain computed from the linearized response. The breadth of the associated response spectrum sets how many near-dangerous pathways coexist beyond the strongest direction. We demonstrate the mechanism in a high-dimensional nonlinear non-normal network with the strongest directional gain held fixed: the system with broader response-channel breadth has a larger nonlinear fragility curve, isolating breadth from the worst direction. An independent scalar test in deterministic traffic breakdown confirms the predicted sign: response breadth lowers calibrated jam thresholds once the strongest response is matched, with residual margins screening but never reversing the effect. Response-spectrum breadth thus emerges as a pre-failure coordinate for finite-amplitude fragility beyond the strongest path.

</details>


### [149] [High Resolution Study of the 2D ANNNI Model Using a Two-replica Cluster Algorithm and Population Annealing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00882)
*Shane Keiser,Jon Machta*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文使用种群退火结合双副本团簇算法研究二维ANNNI模型，成功解析了非公度浮动相中尖锐比热峰的序列，并证明该算法比单副本团簇方法或Metropolis算法更高效，其有效性源于能在副本间移动缺陷线组并结合重采样去除缺陷线较多的副本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维轴向次近邻Ising（ANNNI）模型在非公度浮动相中存在尖锐比热峰序列，但现有算法难以有效平衡系统并完全分辨该序列，因此需要开发更高效的算法来研究该模型。

Method: 采用种群退火（population annealing）结合双副本团簇算法（two-replica cluster algorithm），通过比较单副本团簇方法、Metropolis算法与所提算法的平衡效率，并分析缺陷线移动与重采样机制。

Result: 成功完全分辨了有限尺寸非公度浮动相中尖锐比热峰的序列；双副本团簇算法在平衡系统方面显著优于单副本团簇方法和Metropolis算法。

Conclusion: 双副本团簇算法结合种群退火的高效性来源于其在副本间移动缺陷线组的能力，以及种群退火中重采样步骤能淘汰缺陷线较多的副本，从而提升整体采样效率。

Abstract: The axial next-nearest-neighbor Ising (ANNNI) model in two dimensions is studied using population annealing combined with a two-replica cluster algorithm. We are able to fully resolve the sequence of sharp specific heat peaks that characterize the finite-size incommensurate floating phase. We also show that the two-replica cluster algorithm is much more effective in equilibrating the system than either single-replica cluster methods or the Metropolis algorithm when these are combined with population annealing. We argue that effectiveness of the new algorithm is due to its ability to move groups of defect lines between replicas combined with resampling in population annealing, which removes replicas from the population that have larger numbers of defect lines.

</details>


### [150] [Tandem Exclusion Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01568)
*Ngo Phuoc Nguyen Ngoc,Lam Thi Nhung,Huynh Anh Thi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了"串联排斥过程"(TEP)，一种受蚂蚁串联奔跑启发的随机晶格模型。研究证明其平稳测度为吉布斯测度，并利用传递矩阵方法推导了稳态流和空间关联的精确闭式表达式。结果表明，与TASEP相比，TEP的粒子-空穴交换不对称，最大流出现在密度>1/2处，且当q>1时协作动力学可增强输运效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受自然界中蚂蚁串联奔跑行为的启发，研究协作局部跃迁如何影响一维随机晶格模型中的集体输运性质，探索无需信息素介导的长距离通信下协作协调机制对输运效率的影响。

Method: 定义TEP模型，包含两种协作局部跃迁(110→101和101→011)；证明平稳测度为吉布斯测度π ∝ q^{H(η)}，其中q=β/α；运用传递矩阵方法推导稳态流和空间关联的精确闭式表达式。

Result: 稳态流在粒子-空穴交换(ρ↦1-ρ)下不对称，最大流发生在ρ>1/2处(不同于TASEP的对称流ρ(1-ρ))；q>1时协作动力学使流超过TASEP值，产生强空间聚集；q→∞时流趋近J→αρ，对应近乎无约束的集体输运。

Conclusion: 串联协调机制本身即可在中高密度下显著增强集体输运效率，即使缺乏信息素介导的长距离通信也能实现高效输运。

Abstract: We introduce the \emph{tandem exclusion process} (TEP), a one-dimensional stochastic lattice model motivated by tandem running in ants. Particles evolve through two cooperative local transitions, $110\to101$ at rate $α$ (leader advancement) and $101\to011$ at rate $β$ (follower recovery). We prove that the stationary measure on the dynamically active sector is the Gibbs measure $π\propto q^{H(η)}$, where $q=β/α$ and $H(η)$ counts neighboring occupied pairs, and derive exact closed-form expressions for the stationary current and spatial correlations using transfer-matrix methods. The current is asymmetric under particle--hole exchange $ρ\mapsto1-ρ$, with its maximum occurring at densities strictly larger than $1/2$, in contrast to the symmetric current $ρ(1-ρ)$ of the totally asymmetric simple exclusion process (TASEP). For $q>1$, cooperative dynamics enhances the current above the TASEP value and generates strong spatial clustering; in the limit $q\to\infty$, the current approaches $J\toαρ$, corresponding to nearly unconstrained collective transport. These results suggest that tandem coordination alone can substantially enhance collective transport efficiency at moderate and high densities, even without pheromone-mediated long-range communication.

</details>


### [151] [Significance of time-convolutionless mode-coupling theory in capturing the dynamics of glass-forming liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01696)
*Michio Tokuyama*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文论证了近期提出的时间无卷积模式耦合理论（TMCT）在捕捉玻璃形成液体动力学方面的重要性。从统一视角全面探索了理想MCT与TMCT之间主要差异的根源。首先回顾了海森堡绘景中的两种投影算符方法：Mori的时间卷积（TC）公式和Tokuyama-Mori的时间无卷积（TCL）公式。研究表明，这两种框架的适当选择根本上取决于相关变量的时空尺度。在TMCT中，TC公式应用于电流密度，而TCL公式应用于数密度（因为后者在显著更长的时空尺度上运作）。相比之下，理想MCT对两种密度均应用TC公式。因此，TMCT中的控制方程是无时间卷积的，而理想MCT方程则具有时间卷积形式。这一根本差异显著影响玻璃转变附近的各种物理量。最后，将完整的TMCT方程转换为简化递归方程以促进数值分析，并将TMCT的关键预测与理想MCT进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示TMCT在描述玻璃形成液体动力学中的重要性，并厘清TMCT与理想MCT之间根本差异的理论根源，尤其是在投影算符方法选择及其对时空尺度依赖性的理解上。

Method: 采用理论推导与对比分析方法：回顾海森堡绘景中两种投影算符方法（Mori的TC公式与Tokuyama-Mori的TCL公式）；基于相关变量时空尺度确定公式选择原则；对电流密度应用TC公式、对数密度应用TCL公式构建TMCT；将完整TMCT方程转化为简化递归方程以便数值分析。

Result: 明确了TMCT与理想MCT的根本区别在于：TMCT对电流密度和数密度分别采用TC和TCL公式（基于时空尺度差异），而理想MCT对两者均采用TC公式。这导致TMCT控制方程为时间无卷积形式，理想MCT为时间卷积形式，该差异显著影响玻璃转变附近的物理量。

Conclusion: TMCT通过合理选择投影算符方法，提供了比理想MCT更准确的玻璃形成液体动力学描述。将TMCT方程简化为递归方程有利于数值实现，其关键预测与理想MCT存在显著差异。

Abstract: This paper demonstrates the significance of the recently proposed time-convolutionless mode-coupling theory (TMCT) in capturing the dynamics of glass-forming liquids. The origin of the primary differences between ideal MCT and TMCT is comprehensively explored from a unified perspective. First, we review two distinct projection operator methods in the Heisenberg picture: the time-convolution (TC) formulation proposed by Mori and the time-convolutionless (TCL) formulation proposed by Tokuyama and Mori. We show that the appropriate choice between these frameworks fundamentally depends on the space-time scales of the relevant variables. In TMCT, the TC formulation is applied to the current density, whereas the TCL formulation is applied to the number density because the latter operates on a significantly longer space-time scale. In contrast, ideal MCT applies the TC formulation to both densities. Consequently, the governing equation in TMCT is timeconvolutionless, whereas the ideal MCT equation features a time-convolution form. This fundamental difference significantly affects various physical quantities near the glass transition. Finally, the full TMCT equation is transformed into a simplified recursion equation to facilitate numerical analysis, and the key predictions of TMCT are compared with those of ideal MCT

</details>


### [152] [Role of System-Bath Interaction in Non-Markovian Quantum Brownian Otto Cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01750)
*Haena Shim,Joonhyun Yeo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过精确求解Caldeira-Leggett模型的Heisenberg-Langevin方程，研究了非马尔可夫强耦合效应对有限时间量子Otto循环的影响，发现系统-浴相互作用能在等容过程中对功和热均有贡献，且会降低引擎的功输出，使其功率-效率关系低于马尔可夫极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多采用马尔可夫近似（Lindblad方程）描述量子Otto循环，忽略了非马尔可夫及强耦合效应。本文旨在通过精确解析方法研究这些效应对量子热机循环热力学性质的影响。

Method: 以谐振子为工作物质，采用Caldeira-Leggett模型描述量子布朗运动；通过精确解析求解Heisenberg-Langevin方程研究系统变量及系统-浴相互作用能的动力学，获得循环稳态并与Lindblad方程描述的马尔可夫极限进行对比。

Result: 非马尔可夫强耦合下，等容过程中系统-浴相互作用能的变化同时贡献于功和热；当Otto循环作为热机运行时，相互作用能会降低功输出；非马尔可夫引擎在给定效率下的功率低于马尔可夫功率-效率界。

Conclusion: 系统-浴相互作用能是决定量子热机循环热力学行为的关键因素；非马尔可夫强耦合效应会削弱Otto引擎的性能，使其无法达到马尔可夫近似下的功率-效率最优折衷。

Abstract: We study finite-time quantum Otto cycles whose working medium is a harmonic oscillator undergoing a quantum Brownian motion described by the Caldeira-Leggett model when the oscillator is in contact with heat baths in isochoric processes. The time evolution of the Otto cycle is studied by analytically solving the exact Heisenberg-Langevin equations for the system variables and the interaction energy between the system and the bath. This enables us to investigate non-Markovian strong-coupling effects on the quantum Otto cycle. We obtain cyclic steady states and study the thermodynamic properties of the Otto cycle for various values of the parameters describing the heat baths and the coupling between the system and the bath. We compare our results with those obtained in the Markovian limit, where the time evolution is described by the Lindblad equation. We find that the change in the interaction energy during the isochoric process contributes to both work and heat, and plays a crucial role in determining thermodynamic behavior of the cycle. In particular, we find that when the Otto cycle operates as an engine, the effect of the interaction energy is to reduce the work output. We also compare our results with the power-efficiency trade-off relation recently proposed for the Markovian quantum Otto engine. We find that the power of our non-Markovian engine for a given efficiency value falls below the Markovian power-efficiency bound.

</details>


### [153] [Can the Brownian diffusion coefficient be reconstructed from Lyapunov exponents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01887)
*I. G. Marchenko,I. I. Marchenko,D. Ivashchenko,J. Łuczka,J. Spiechowicz*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了零温下非混沌的交流驱动粒子在周期势中的运动，发现非零温下正常扩散系数与确定性系统的最大Lyapunov指数之间存在准周期关联，并提出了一个近似公式来重建扩散系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Lyapunov指数与布朗扩散之间不存在一般性关系，但近期研究表明扩散系数是驱动振幅的准周期函数。作者希望探索能否从确定性系统（零温）的最大Lyapunov指数重建非零温下的准周期扩散系数。

Method: 在零温极限下分析非混沌动力学，然后在非零温下观察正常布朗扩散。通过数值模拟比较扩散系数与对应的确定性系统最大Lyapunov指数的关系，提出一个用于重建扩散系数的近似公式，并测试其在不同系统参数下的鲁棒性。

Result: 提出的近似公式能够准确重建非零温下的准周期扩散系数，与数值模拟吻合良好，但在扩散系数局部极大值附近存在一些偏差。

Conclusion: 扩散系数与Lyapunov指数之间存在可用的关联性，该近似公式在参数变化下具有一定的鲁棒性。

Abstract: We consider an ac-driven particle moving in a spatially periodic and symmetric potential. In the zero- temperature limit, for the analyzed parameter set, its dynamics is non-chaotic and the particle does not manifest diffusive properties. At non-zero temperatures, the asymptotic long-time motion follows normal (Brownian) diffusion. Recent studies have shown that within tailored parameter regimes, the diffusion coef- ficient is a quasiperiodic function of the external driving amplitude [1]. Although no general relation between Lyapunov exponents and Brownian diffusion exists, we demonstrate that the quasiperiodic diffusion coefficient at non-zero temperature can be accurately reconstructed from the maximal Lyapunov exponent of the corresponding deterministic system (at vanishing temperature). We propose an approximate formula for this purpose, which shows good agreement with numerical simulations, although some discrepancies are detected in the vicinity of the local maxima of the diffusion coefficient. Finally, we examine the robustness of the correlation between diffusion and the Lyapunov exponent under variations of the system parameters.

</details>


### [154] [Linear optimal protocol for physical constraints in weakly driven processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01988)
*Pierre Nazé*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性响应理论中弱驱动系统不可逆功的最小化问题，发现全局最优解为零模式，对应恒定驱动速度和线性协议，最优功仅取决于积分松弛函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弱驱动系统的线性响应框架下，如何在物理约束（协议导数受限）下最小化不可逆功，是一个基础且重要的理论问题。

Method: 将问题归结为涉及松弛函数的移位本征值方程；利用松弛核在对称区间上的周期性表示恢复连续平移不变性；在傅里叶基下对角化算子，求解全局最优解。

Result: 全局最优解为零模式，即恒定驱动速度下的线性协议；最优功仅依赖于积分松弛函数；遗传编程数值结果验证了该解在不同核函数下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 线性响应下不可逆功最小化的最优策略是线性驱动协议，该结论具有跨核函数的普适性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: The minimization of irreversible work in weakly driven systems within linear response under physical constraints on the protocol derivative is studied. The problem reduces to a shifted eigenvalue equation involving the relaxation function. Owing to its dependence on time differences and its evenness, the relaxation kernel is naturally defined over a symmetric interval, where a periodic representation arises as a consistent closure that restores continuous translational invariance. Also, it shows how the irreversible work is defined in practice. Within this framework, the operator becomes diagonal in a Fourier basis. The global optimal solution is the zero mode, yielding a constant driving speed and a linear protocol. The corresponding optimal work depends only on the integrated relaxation function. Numerical results obtained via genetic programming confirm the robustness of this solution across different kernels.

</details>


### [155] [Electromagnetic Field Equivalence from Moving Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02187)
*David V. Svintradze*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文从运动流形上的电磁场几何表述出发，推导了动态隔离区域间的场等效条件。通过引入电磁场张量构造能量密度，建立了Maxwell电动力学的几何推广，并证明经典Maxwell方程是该广义演化系统的一个约束几何扇区。在场等效条件下，场差满足无源Maxwell方程，且平衡Maxwell扇区建立了Lorentz不变电磁结构与常平均曲率流形之间的直接对应关系。静态弯曲构型通过曲率贡献保持非零电磁对比，而动态演化流形则允许非平凡等效扇区，但静态Euclidean有界构型被排除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将电磁场在运动流形上的几何表述应用于动态分离域之间的场等效问题，探索Maxwell电动力学与微分几何之间更深层的结构对应关系。

Method: 采用运动超曲面的张量不变运动方程，通过电磁场张量构造能量密度，建立Maxwell电动力学的几何扩展；通过比较内部与外部电磁构型，在无界面电流交换的隔离条件下分析场差方程，并引入常平均曲率流形建立电磁-几何对应。

Result: 经典Maxwell方程是广义演化系统的约束几何扇区；场差满足无源Maxwell方程；平衡扇区建立了电磁结构与常平均曲率流形的对应；非平凡等效场存在于动态演化流形的特定速度扇区，但静态Euclidean有界构型被排除。

Conclusion: 动态场等效条件需要流形演化维持，静态弯曲构型通过曲率诱导贡献保留电磁对比，这为理解弯曲时空中的电磁等效原理提供了几何基础。

Abstract: We apply a geometric formulation of electromagnetic fields on moving manifolds to the problem of field equivalence between dynamically separated domains. Starting from the tensorially invariant equations of motion for moving hypersurfaces, we introduce an electromagnetic specialization by constructing an energy density from the electromagnetic field tensor, yielding a geometric extension of Maxwell electrodynamics. The classical Maxwell equations then emerge as a constrained geometric sector of the broader evolution system. Hence, by comparing internal and external electromagnetic configurations, we show that under isolation conditions with no interfacial current exchange, the field difference satisfies the source-free Maxwell equation. Furthermore, the equilibrium Maxwell sector establishes a direct correspondence between the Lorentz-invariant electromagnetic structure and the geometry of constant-mean-curvature manifolds. The resulting field-difference equations admit equivalence solutions generated by specific velocity sectors of the moving-manifold dynamics. We further demonstrate that the resulting field-equivalence regime is intrinsically dynamical: static curved configurations generically retain nonvanishing electromagnetic contrast through curvatureinduced contributions to the geometric pressure balance, whereas dynamically evolving manifolds admit nontrivial admissible sectors satisfying the equivalence condition. Explicit nonvacuum realizations are obtained within the tangential-flow sector of the moving-manifold system, while bounded static Euclidean configurations are generically excluded.

</details>
